 const OFFICIAL_UPDATE_TOOLS = {"version":"official-refresh-v1","instructions":"# Official data refresh\n\nThis Site is private to its owner. The official-data import endpoint relies on that Sites access boundary and must not be exposed to a shared or public audience. Routine updates persist observations without deploying code.\n\nFor a cloud run, use Sites `get_site` for `appgprj_6ab2cc2055ac8191a2244d934f7384ee`. Confirm the owner-only audience and retrieve the current service bypass bearer token. Keep it only in memory; send `OAI-Sites-Authorization: Bearer <token>` for private service HTTP calls. This grants no signed-in visitor identity and must never be used to impersonate one. GET `/api/official-data-tools` from the returned deployed origin to retrieve this saved revision's instructions, `collectorPython` and `importerNode`. Write only these credential-free scripts to scratch. A data refresh needs no Git credentials, source edits or deployment.\n\n1. Read `/api/market-scanner` first. Run the retrieved Python collector with `--output <scratch-directory> --sessions 60` when fewer than 60 sessions are saved; otherwise use `--sessions 1` to append or replace the latest official session. It downloads official FRED CSV observations, their matching latest observation and next release date from FRED's official series pages, the TWSE holiday calendar/FMTQIK trading dates and same-day MI_MARGN, MI_MARGN_TREND and BFIJ3U raw statistics, the current TWSE/TPEx issuer roster, and date-matched TWSE MI_INDEX / TPEx OTC quotes. The latest date comes from the actual MI_INDEX after-trading report, because STOCK_DAY_ALL can lag after market close. `--macro-only` refreshes the FRED observations and market statistics without recollecting stock history. FRED provider failures are isolated by series; read `macro-collection-status.json` and report remaining failures. FinMind public metadata supplies industry labels only. Retain raw sources and collection status for review. Do not mix TPEx OTC quotes with fixed-price after-hours quotes, or replace a rejected historical date with the latest day. Current classifications do not establish a historical survivorship-corrected universe.\n2. Launch `stty -echo` then `node <retrieved-importer.mjs> <scratch-directory>` in a PTY. At the hidden-stdin prompt supply a single JSON line with the current `token` and the deployed `origin` from `get_site`. Do not include credentials in arguments, files, logs or task text. The importer posts the universe, observations and dated snapshots, then reads saved status. Abort on a rejected write; do not claim a successful update based only on collection. Existing company evidence remains saved; import newly verified filings separately when available.\n3. POST `/api/automation/refresh-all`, poll `/api/automation/status` at 10-second intervals until the matching run has `completedAt`, and read `/api/automation/history`. GET `/api/macro`, `/api/macro-expanded` and `/api/master-model?id=8299` to verify source observation dates, usable coverage, scanner session count and eligible history. An initial cached response may start a background refresh; read again after completion.\n4. Preserve the existing macro score rules, raw financial warnings, candidate-only research and Gate criteria. Verify company filings separately before importing additional evidence through `companies`; published actual EPS is not an advance forecast or pre-announcement consensus. Eight distinct verified periods and direct-peer estimates remain required by the relevant Gates. Report unavailable sources and incomplete checks explicitly.\n\nThe Worker attempts the same official quote basis during refresh when the imported roster is fresh. The external collector is the fallback for Worker egress failures. Holiday/no-trade observations, suspended securities and newly listed stocks remain excluded or ineligible rather than filled with invented values. No Gemini or model-generated values are involved.\n\n## Core CPI collection and readback\n\nThe same official collector now writes `cpi.json` from BLS `cu.data.0.Current`\n(seasonally adjusted `CUSR0000SA0L1E`) and FRED's public `CPILFESL` CSV.\n`cu.data.1.AllItems` does not contain the core series. The Worker and external\ncollector stream the large BLS file and retain only the core monthly history in\nthe import payload; the external collector also keeps its raw source file.\nRoutine collection includes both sources; `--cpi-only` retries only this step.\nIf the BLS file is blocked, the external collector retries the same series\nthrough the keyless BLS v1 API, preserving the failed file attempt. These two\nBLS routes still count as one institution, and cannot pass without FRED.\nRead `cpi-collection-status.json` as well as the macro collection status. A failed\nsource remains an error, with no copied or fabricated observation. An empty\n`cpi.json` records source failures instead of reusing a stale payload file.\n\nThe existing importer automatically submits `cpi.json`. The server validates the\nexact series/source, capture age, monthly dates and consecutive calculation\nwindow, then independently checks the latest month and value. BLS and FRED must\nmatch the same month within 0.02 index points. A one-month tolerance must never\nturn different periods into agreement; two BLS endpoints are one source family.\nOne usable source or a month/value mismatch is imported as unverified, with its\ndated history and failures retained. Inspect `coreCpi` in the import result and\n`CPI_READBACK`, then GET `/api/core-cpi-history` and `/api/macro` after refresh.\nOnly a verified, current observation may support CPI-derived macro readings.\nImports clear CPI response caches; the latest source observation takes priority\nover a lagging endpoint. Existing monthly observation/capture/audit limits stay\nin force. The scheduled run already retrieves these saved scripts; no additional\nschedule or manual CPI seed redeployment is required.\n\nEach CPI import also saves a durable generation marker. Macro requests compare\nit with their cached generation, including across Worker isolates. A request\nstarts the normal background rebuild, or adopts an already rebuilt D1 snapshot;\nit never labels an older calculation as updated. Jobs begun before the import\nretain their original generation, so late completion cannot suppress a refresh.\n\nThe cross-market and US-sector automation adapters wrap their parsed snapshots\nin HTTP Responses before passing them to the common module runner. A successful\nrefresh must save both modules' real coverage; source failures still produce\npartial/error states. This repair changes no dimension weights, total-score\nrules, VETO thresholds, Gate approvals or risk budgets.\n## Automatic company and industry sources\n\nSwitching stocks or refreshing the selected stock calls `/api/stock-source-research?id=CODE`.\nEach symbol has its own durable source pool. Company announcements are matched by exact\nissuer code; industry RSS entries are matched by the configured product/industry profile.\nShared source feeds are cached for six hours. Missing provider coverage, invalid dates,\nempty company announcements and upstream errors remain explicit gaps. RSS forecasts and\nsame-industry peer candidates never become EPS consensus or completed backtests.\n\nThe deployed `/api/official-data-tools` bundle also contains `sourceCollectorNode`.\nSave that exact string to a temporary `.mjs` file and run it with the payload folder as\nits only argument, after the existing official collector. The private importer picks up\n`sources.json` automatically. This external route refreshes the same source cache when\nan official endpoint cannot be reached by the Worker. It needs no model API or secrets.\nRecord `source-collection-status.json` errors; successful feeds do not hide failed feeds.\nThe existing daily site refresh collects the source pool for every stock in that run's\nwatchlist batch, including when the financial snapshot is still fresh.\n\nPublic verified company evidence is evaluated per symbol. Optional manual records\nremain isolated per authenticated account; service access never supplies a user ID.\nGate 5 counts eight distinct periods for one guidance metric and eight distinct\npre-earnings consensus/result pairs. Gate 8 requires current and next-year source\nconsensus plus three verified direct peers with matching-date TWD prices; shared\nindustry feeds, model EPS and repeated brokers cannot satisfy these conditions.\nDCF output remains a scenario until its actual Rf/Beta/Kd parameters are measured.\nNonpositive normalized FCFF is a model applicability limit, with the cycle valuation\nshown separately. Financial warnings remain warnings after data wiring is repaired.\n\nThe official market collector preserves `noTradeRows` for explicit zero volume and\n`volumeRows` for known volume when close/change fields cannot populate a full quote.\nNeither creates a missing closing price. RVOL20 uses the last 21 aligned official\nsessions; unknown volume is never zero-filled. The official issuer roster supplies\nlisting dates so new-listing warmup can be distinguished from actual missing days.\nMarket-level RVOL keeps a 95% threshold over the mature population. Only new listings with a verified official listing date, fewer than 21 expected sessions and no actual missing session are excluded from that denominator. Preserve the full-population coverage separately. These excluded new stocks remain individually ineligible for RVOL and selection; an unverified listing or real missing day is never excluded.\n`--issuer-only` refreshes listing metadata; `--market-only` collects only market\nhistory. Imports batch at most five dates within the existing request size limit.\nROIC must refer to the latest statement quarter and use positive known capital at\nboth endpoints; an earlier valid ROIC is retained only as dated historical evidence.\n\nThe importer includes `statistics.json` automatically. The server independently\nchecks the latest official trading date against the holiday calendar, the same-day\ncredit turnover and market capitalization, and matching financing/short balances.\nThese source snapshots expire after 36 hours without another verified retrieval;\nnew trading sessions invalidate an old statistics snapshot even within that window.\nThe five-minute UI refresh interval does not expire valid source observations.\nFailed refreshes retain each valid observation's original verification timestamp.\nEnergy official spot snapshots are selected before futures proxies. A matching\nFRED next-release verification can retain the latest published observation through\nthat release date, subject to a fresh retrieval and a bounded 21-day release gap.\n\n## Optional metadata failure isolation\n\nFinMind company labels are optional. HTTP 402 or other metadata failures are\nretained in issuerUniverse.metadataErrors and collection-status.json; they no longer\nabort official market collection. TWSE/TPEx industry codes are used as explicit\n`官方產業別 <code>` labels with their original source, without inventing industry\nnames or buying a provider subscription. No quote, date or price basis is changed.\n\n## Gate input wiring (2026-10-16)\n\n`worker/master-inputs.js` connects Gate 4 to stored evidence without assigning grades.\n\n* S3 reads verified `peer_comparison` relations and `veto_inputs` with `metric: moat`.\n  `comparative_claims` entries require peer_symbol, aspect (market_share,\n  switching_cost, technology, pricing_power), statement, source_url and source_date.\n  A sector roster alone is insufficient.\n* S4 reads the stock's dated `marketMultiples.psr`. A `metric: psr` input may supply\n  `history` entries (date, market_cap, revenue_ttm, currency:TWD,\n  basis:consolidated_TTM, source_url) covering the five previous completed years.\n  Parent valuation_date/currency/basis must match. Annual snapshots do not establish\n  the separate five-year-high Veto.\n* S5 reference PEG = current consensus Forward P/E divided by next-year consensus\n  EPS growth in percentage points. Both years must pass source coverage checks.\n  Nonpositive growth is not applicable; no 3–5-year extrapolation or grade is added.\n* S7 reads `metric: capacity`, region/exposure, alternatives and sourced\n  `customer_exposures` (customer, percent, source_url). The existing >80% compound\n  Veto is retained; no utilization threshold is introduced.\n* S8 reads five components from `metric: cb`: dated registry balances, verified\n  current conversion prices, same-date TWD capital, same-date FX, and inventory/\n  put/subsequent events. Original issue prices do not fill current prices.\n  `capital` carries value/currency/date/source_url; `fx` carries date/source_url and\n  `rates` in TWD per foreign-currency unit. Bond `event_review` carries through,\n  TRIGGERED/NOT_TRIGGERED and source_url. Unresolved document dates remain unknown;\n  all-domestic issues require no FX conversion.\n\nGate 5 pairs verified structured documents by company, normalized period, metric,\nunit, currency and accounting basis. Supported results are `quarterly_result` and\n`annual_result`; metrics use existing named objects or a `metrics` array. Guidance\nuses `management_guidance`. Unknown publication dates and conflicting actuals are\nrejected with reasons. Annual/quarterly samples and unlike units never pool.\n\n`quarterly_consensus` is separate from annual `consensus_estimate`: it requires\nsymbol, period, TWD per-share unit, accounting_basis, consensus_eps, an approved\noriginal provider-report URL, source_date and `consensus_captured_at` with time zone.\nThe original snapshot must predate earnings; current downloads never recreate\nhistorical knowledge. Without an earnings time, Taipei midnight is conservative.\nManual guidance also needs actual_source, actual_date, unit and accounting_basis.\nOld incomplete rows remain saved but unevaluable. Browser/server share validators.\n\nGate 8 reads public verified peer consensus and same-date full-market quotes\nwithout prior peer-page visits. Optional manual estimates remain account scoped.\nProduction relations require symbol/peer_symbol, direct_peer/production_eligible,\nVERIFIED_SOURCE, source_date/source_url and relationship_basis. Industry candidates\nand foreign-product references do not become three TWD peers. Per-peer audits show\nactual date/coverage failures; missing provider coverage remains missing.\n\nFull-site refresh saves and reads back per-stock model snapshots under\n`site-master-audit:v1:<runId>:<symbol>`. Status/history expose gateAudits and\ngateAuditCount; recorded means storage succeeded, not Gate passage. No additional\nschedule, subscription or automatic trade is created. Original score rules remain.\n\n## Master remediation and public consensus\n\nGate 4 evaluates the original five compound Veto redlines separately from financial warnings. The cash/ROE redline pairs four-quarter consolidated ROE with CFO/net income from exactly the same quarters; a negative three-year CFO alone does not trigger it. Unknown conditions stay pending. Explicit sourced grading criteria, dimension weights and 1–4 grades are required for 10D scoring. Known group weights are 35/30/25/10; the independent company/ownership/entry-risk layers are 40/30/30. Do not invent the unrecovered grade cutoffs or within-group weights.\n\nVerified FactSet reports published by Cnyes may be imported as `consensus_estimate` company evidence, with exact symbol, year, TWD range, publication date and analyst count. An unreported next-year analyst count is null, retained as a source-verified candidate, and never borrowed from the current year. These public records are read separately from each visitor’s private notes and cannot create quarterly surprise history.\n\nWhen FCFF DCF is inapplicable and the cycle alternative passes all sample/date/product checks, the header, scenario cards, report and valuation quadrant use that cycle alternative as the primary valuation. The current-year forecast remains explicitly separate. ROE uses consolidated income and total consolidated equity; OBV requires actual volume observations without filling unknown volume with zero.\n# 209：有限觀察報告與手機診斷\n\n原始 `status`、Gate 的 `blocksOverall`、一票否決及正式評分條件保持獨立。\n核心 Gate 1／2／3／7／9／10 與必要財報欄位核驗後，`outputStatus=qualified`\n可產生明列限制的暫定觀察報告；`qualifiedOutput.isInvestmentRating=false`、總分為 null。\n資料不足的維度不補中性分數，指引／季度共識未達八期標記為 `insufficient_sample`。\n已核實的今年共識提供獨立 Forward P/E 參考；明年覆蓋與同業不足保留待核，\n沒有可追溯同口徑次產業資料時不生成替代中位數。未知否決條件不放行，觸發否決則剔除／0 分。\n\n前端請求改用 AbortController 與可清理計時器，涵蓋回應本文的逾時，避免依賴\n舊手機可能缺少的 AbortSignal.timeout。渲染失敗保留可見狀態與重新載入操作。\n頁尾診斷只收集裝置類型、視窗尺寸、API 路徑類別、耗時、HTTP 狀態及錯誤類別；\n不收集研究內容、原始 User-Agent、查詢參數或憑證。最多 48 筆，保存間隔至少一分鐘。\n`GET/POST /api/client-diagnostics` 依網站提供的使用者身分隔離；未登入回 401，\n已保存診斷以 30 天有效期顯示。長任務觀測不支援時明列限制，不把空白記錄當成實機通過。\n單元測試的 iOS／Android 類型僅驗證分類，不能當作實機證據。\n\nUS3M／US10Y／US30Y 的官方外部資料路徑分別校驗 DTB3／DGS10／DGS30。\n官方 CSV 與 FRED 頁面的最新日期、值相同，且下一次發布日期與核對時間通過驗證時，\n依發布窗口保留最新觀測；原觀測日不變，下一次發布或核對有效期屆滿後重新待核。\n收集器新增這三個序列，匯入器每次最多提交五個序列，維持原子的來源驗證。\n# 210：自訂退避風險評級與前端熔斷\n\n使用者指定資料不足仍產出判讀。本版新增 `adaptiveRating`，名稱為\n「自訂退避風險評級」，與原始 10D／三層審計分數分開。`status=degraded`、\n`outputStatus=graded` 表示已產出退避評級，`auditStatus` 保留原始條件狀態。\n未填補的原始分數仍為 null，未核實否決不變成安全；已觸發紅線優先 VETO／剔除。\n\n退避級距規則：現價 ≤ Bear 為 A、≤ Base 為 B+、≤ Bull 為 B、超過 Bull 為 C。\n這是自訂政策分類，不是阮慕驊 App 的原始公式。Gate 4 分级缺漏採中立、貢獻權重0；\nGate 5 任一八期樣本未齊則指引權重0、最高B+，已核驗當年共識參考權重乘0.85。\n15% 是使用者指定的政策折減，不是統計準確率，也不改寫原始 EPS 或情境價格。\nGate 8 缺共識／直接同業時採自身已核驗歷史 P/E，列樣本區間及筆數；無法取得的\nP/B 或次產業中位數不填造。財報／治理警訊扣一級，同源 CFO 與 FCF 不重複扣減；\n價格低於 MA20 與 MA60 再扣一級。未知否決條件最高B，核心或財報資料不齊最高C。\n除明確 VETO 以外，這些限制不再阻止產出已讀取資料的風險觀察。\n\n前後端使用同一份 `worker/fallback-rating.js`，由 build 嵌入頁面，避免公式分歧。\n模型請求五秒仍未完成時，以目前個股已核驗、日期有效的行情與估值輸出本機退避結果；\n後台有獨立逾時保護，回來後更新為服務端結果。過期或無行情／無有效情境時不生成價格評級。\n\n相同 GET 請求共用一次網路讀取，返回可獨立使用的 Response。連續三次網路或服務失敗\n開啟熔斷，401／403／404 不盲重試；只有使用者重新查詢才重置。市場、總經、個股、\n補充、新聞與資料池的待完成輪詢各有三次自動重試上限，週期計時器不能繞過上限。\n同一動畫幀的重繪合併，同步重入不再遞迴。手機 Gate 詳情、來源、總經全指標、\n監測維度與核對明細延後至展開才建立；長清單每批20筆，由使用者按鈕追加。\n手機的補充總經與新聞載入延後500ms。巡檢狀態不再直接覆寫模型 Gate 3 內容。\n\n驗證包含缺樣本分級、真實否決優先、五秒本機結果、100次更新只重繪一次、\n1000筆來源的延後／分批渲染、熔斷與週期輪詢上限。這些是程式層檢查，不能冒稱實機驗證。\n\n# 211：快取期限與收盤後更新\n\n個股快照從 D1 載入執行環境時只保留原期限剩餘時間，不再重新延長六小時；\n已過期快照不寫入新鮮快取。巡檢同樣依原始 D1 有效期判斷，不能把短期限失敗快照\n當成六小時內仍可用。既有排程保留啟用狀態，改於台灣 06:30、20:30 執行，\n包含盤後行情及晚間信用交易統計。排程保存不代表未來每次來源必定成功。\n\n官方收集器以 MI_INDEX 最新報表的實際觀測日決定最新日期，仍驗證兩市場同日、\n普通股口徑及最低覆蓋率。已有60個交易日的資料庫只增量抓取最新一日；\n`--sessions 1` 不再額外抓取十二日。缺日及新掛牌暖機仍保留待核。\nGate 3 以最新已取得且日期有效的行情核對掃描交易日；晚間信用／市場結構另列日期，\n其前一交易日快照不阻止已核實的新盤後掃描。過期、未來行情不參與日期核驗，\n較舊掃描仍維持待更新；Gate 2 信用欄位與發布窗口規則保留獨立核驗。\n\n# 212：估值口徑透明與 CPI 核驗時鐘\n\n退避報告直接列歷史 EPS 年度區間、Base EPS × 歷史 P/E 中位數的算式，\n以及與當年共識 EPS 的倍數差異。原始10D／三層總分未完成核驗時直接標示。\n維持既有退避級距及真實風險，不以增加或刪除資料警告改寫分級。\n`forecastFactor=0.85` 是保留的使用者政策係數；本版明示\n`consensusUsage=reference_only`、`forecastCoefficientApplied=false`。\n當年共識 EPS／Forward P/E 仍為旁列原值，未與歷史 EPS 加權或改變本次D分級。\n歷史情境的核驗範圍是來源及算術，不宣称本輪可持續獲利或目標價已驗證。\n\nCPI 原始月度來源擷取期限30日、統計月有效期75日，以及BLS／FRED同月同值\n核驗期限12小時各自檢查。兩來源必須來自不同官方來源機構，数值差≤0.02點；\n保留原始擷取日，使用真實核驗日判斷新鮮度。CPI核驗屆滿即重新查核，\n原始指數及3月年化／年增衍生值同時降為歷史參考。API新鮮快取與決策鏈\n共用相同核驗函式，不能因快取尚未到期而把過期來源算成有效。\n\n# 214：研究摘要與同日利率診斷\n\n既有研究來源保留 RSS description 的原始摘要、來源網址、發布日與讀取範圍。\n`content_version=2` 會在下一次自動收集時更新舊版僅含標題的快取；公司紀錄、\n資料庫鍵值及既有巡檢排程保留。抓取失敗保留原始日期並列為舊參考；沒有摘要\n時只展示標題與來源，不宣稱已閱讀全文。摘要進入 Master Model 原有最終報告，\n按股票資料池與產品分類篩選，同一網址去重，最多引用三筆。研究資料不替代\n公司指引、財報前 EPS 共識或全市場成交證據；不增加分數，也不清除 VETO。\n\n利率診斷沿用既有 FRED DGS10（站內 US10Y）、DFII10、T10YIE 管線，保存\n最近100筆來源歷史，取共同觀測日核對名目減實質與BEI，容許差0.02百分點。\n每項最新日期仍獨立保留，不能把較新的BEI日期套到較舊的名目或實質殖利率。\n來源、單位、日期、有效期或歷史值衝突時顯示UNKNOWN；同日算式不一致時WATCH。\n這是同源資料的一致性診斷，不是獨立來源核驗，也不是新交易訊號或風控放行。\nOpenBB 備援維持關閉；沒有提供特定 OpenBB 報告內容時不生成假報告摘要。\n\n2026-10-14 relay實測：三序列各20筆，19個共同日期；最新共同日2026-10-09，\n5.24% − 2.91% = 2.33%，BEI單項最新日2026-10-13。浮點誤差近零。\n測試涵蓋日期錯位、無交集、過期、未來值、錯誤序列、單位與重複值，RSS摘要、\n股票切換、去重、舊快取更新，以及新增參考前後評分／VETO與必要Gate狀態一致。\n手機驗證為390px DOM模擬及原HTML/CSS雜湊相同；未執行真實裝置瀏覽器檢查。\n\n# 215：GitHub 正式個股行情接入原站\n\n`worker/stock-relay.js` 從 macro-relay-service 的 `data/stock_snapshots_v2.json`\n讀取正式資料池，沿用現有行情與 Master Model 元件，不掛載外部 embed 的新面板。\n驗證各代號、官方網址、日期、數值及擷取有效期；收盤、成交股數、成交金額分別保留，\n缺欄位為 null／UNKNOWN，不能用零代替。單一股票驗證失敗不隱藏其他股票。\n資料池不提供 EPS／ROIC，這些資料仍由原有財報與研究流程獨立取得。\n\n後台共用一次 HTTP 讀取（2.5秒逾時），以既有 D1 快取保存。網頁快照快取命中時\n仍能接入新的官方收盤，保持原財報 checkedAt 及期限；首次讀取在背景進行並使用\n既有有上限的輪詢。FinMind 的歷史行情、月營收、財報與股票切換流程保留。\n較新原站行情不被較舊資料池回退，較新持久化行情也由單一條件式 SQL 防止晚到\n請求覆寫；同日價格衝突保留原值並列 UNKNOWN。fetchStatus 與 publishStatus 分列，\n成功執行可能是 UPDATED／UNCHANGED／RETAINED_NEWER，而非一定發布新觀測。\n\n行情更新先合併歷史再計算 MA／OBV、股價相關倍數及股權折現；舊版快照缺少原始\n歷史或 DCF 輸入時先明列缺值，並背景重讀。缺少中間交易紀錄不補造價量。\n金融輸入未齊時保留來源與其他已取得資料，不製造 EPS 共識或解除 VETO。\n報告與行情 API 使用相同來源選擇，前端把收盤日期／數值納入模型版本辨識，\n拒絕延遲的舊價格報告。正式來源資訊顯示在原報告及 Gate 10 既有來源清單。\n\n實際 GitHub 資料包（blob 21037b07b2c712a3ebf202f53bd2b91e0408073f）四檔均為\n2026-10-13 官方收盤，包含成交股數與金額。測試使用原始包，不改寫上游資料池；\n另以明確標示的測試資料驗證 10/12 不覆蓋 10/13、來源／日期／代號拒絕、SQLite\n併發寫入防護、價格一致、股權折現重算、歷史缺口及既有390px報告流程。\n\n# 216：美股11板塊價格輪動接入原站\n\n沿用 macro-relay-service commit 4c449f99c62dac82f73f5273315c30122aa4e7ac 的四個\n純運算模組，保存在 `worker/us-sector-core.js` 的獨立作用域；新增\n`worker/us-sectors.js` 直接讀取 `data/us_sector_history.json`，無需另部署外部 Worker。\nSPY 作基準，XLK、XLC、XLY、XLP、XLF、XLI、XLV、XLE、XLB、XLU、XLRE 為板塊\nETF。5／10／20D 價格報酬差使用百分點；包含ETF追蹤誤差，未含配息與匯率。\n輪動分類僅表示ETF相對價格，不當作成分股廣度、資金流、EPS修正或ROIC。\n\n既有 `/api/master-model` 報告包含 `report.usSectorContext` 與\n`crossMarket.usSectorContext`；獨立核對路由為 `/api/us-sector-report`。\n來源檢查日期、重複、正值、同一21交易日窗口、擷取有效期、紐約收盤完成時間。\n台股報告只用台股基準日前已完成的美股收盤；保留來源最新日與分析日兩個欄位，\n不把10/13美股收盤塞入10/13台股盤後截點。整體與每個板塊分列覆蓋率及UNKNOWN。\n\n資料使用既有 D1 保存15分鐘快取，條件式SQL防止較舊SPY觀測日覆寫較新快照。\n網路逾時2.5秒，首次取數與過期更新在背景執行；失敗保留原始擷取日並重新驗證。\n未知板塊資料不新增阻擋，不改原Macro Gate、10D、VETO或原始分數，貢獻0。\n既有06:30／20:30巡檢加入板塊收集，不新增排程。原報告摘要及Gate1來源／詳情\n展示ETF相對報酬，HTML/CSS保持原樣；已有板塊背景更新時可繞過一次模型快取重讀。\n\nQA使用原始12檔、各65筆的GitHub資料（blob 28f0166c173f063e281af1c492a261986001753a），\n獨立Python核對66個相對報酬，驗證缺日、錯日、未收盤、來源失敗、去重、保留較新\n資料、D1報告路由、分數/VETO不變，以及390px下UNKNOWN報告持續顯示。\n這些是程式及資料包測試；部署成功不冒稱已完成實機或正式站API的獨立網路讀取。\n\n# 217：修正11板塊在首頁不可見\n\n216 已有12檔來源及11板塊運算，但只有報告摘要／Gate1長文字；手機長文字預設\n收合，首頁也未獨立讀取板塊 API。新增原總經區內的「美股11大板塊」表，位置在\n美國主要股指下方；預設顯示11列及5D／10D／20D相對SPY報酬（百分點）。\n本表採最新已完成美股收盤，個股報告保留原台股基準日對齊。顯示分析日、來源\n最新日及擷取時間；來源未知、單一板塊缺值／錯日保留UNKNOWN，其他板塊與股票\n照常呈現。開頁獨立GET `/api/us-sector-report`，15分鐘前景刷新、立即更新與有\n上限的背景重試均接入；不等待個股財報，也不新增分數或變更Macro Gate／VETO。\n\n樣式限於新增表格，既有HTML／CSS扣除新區塊後仍比對原v212雜湊。390px DOM\n檢查11列可見、33個相對報酬、日期、缺值、背景完成、失敗保留及重試上限。\n沒有使用實機或瀏覽器渲染工具；不把DOM測試宣稱為實機畫面驗收。\n\n# 218：市場日期稽核與VETO證據補查\n\n市場9欄位新增日期稽核，分列資料所屬日、來源發布時間（未知為null）與擷取時間。\n同日比較只取實際存在的共同觀測；不得前填日期，也不把本次歷史資料下載宣稱為\n當年已知的回測資料。未提供發布時刻時以首次觀測標示資訊可得性限制。\n原Gate2的逐項資料核對與原市場卡顯示日期及落後原因，無新增頁面或總分。\n\n修正較舊relay直接覆蓋較新官方融資／融券資料；加入最新盤後MI_INDEX對照，\n避免OpenAPI停在前一交易日。來源錯誤保留原日期，不誤報為當日尚未發布。\nCNN情緒卡同時顯示來源觀測時間及網站核對時間，以辨別同值更新與舊快照。\n\n群聯可轉債條款由2026H1官方財報註6(14)核對並保存標準匯入包，分開發行面額、\n期末帳面負債、初始轉換價及持有人賣回日；目前轉換價與最新存量未核對，仍為\n部分官方證據，不解除CB未知或VETO封頂。過期個股資料的VETO已核對數歸零。\n\n`scripts/collect-veto-pledge.py`按股票代號取得MOPS官方CSV，保存資料年月、出表日、\n實際擷取時間及SHA256，保留董監明細與合計表不同資料期。群聯9月明細出表為\n10/15；官方合計表仍為8/19的7.81%，故本月合計不填入數字、不解除未知。來源\nCSV可下載但TPEx JSON端點回傳非JSON，兩個來源的取數狀況分開記錄。\n# 月度發布與公告備援\n\n信用統計收集也以 MI_INDEX 最新盤後報表日期為錨。FMTQIK OpenAPI 日期落後時，改抓同月官方 FMTQIK JSON，保留該實際來源與原始欄位；伺服器另核對日期、成交金額欄位及信用統計同日值。來源尚未對齊時最多重試三次、間隔十秒，仍失敗則留缺；不得沿用舊 `statistics.json`。可用 `--statistics-only` 單獨重試此段。\n\n紐約新訂單以官方摘要月份及 NOCDISA CSV 同月同值交叉核對，不使用未來新訂單欄。M1B−M2 保留實際觀測月份，另核對央行最新金融情況新聞稿的日平均年增率、發布日及下次預告發布時間；到時失效，不能以延長擷取時間冒充新觀測。\n\n`sourceCollectorNode` 除既有八項公司／產業來源外，另保存 `news.json` 與 `news-collection-status.json`。公告先試 RSS，再試 EIA Today in Energy 與 BLS CPI／就業原始公告 HTML；HTML 必須解析原始發布日，不取下次公布日期。`importerNode` 匯入 `news.json`，官方公告快照最多保留 36 小時擷取有效期。RSS 失敗紀錄保留，HTML 成功不代表數值序列已核驗；BLS 數值 API 與新聞公告用途分開，無須為 RSS 403 假定缺少 API key。\n","collectorPython":"\"\"\"Fetch dated official observations and quotes. rawdir.mkdir(exist_ok=True)\nnow = lambda: dt.datetime.now(dt.timezone.utc).isoformat()\ndef fetch(url):\n    error = None\n    for _ in range(2):\n        try:\n            return urllib.request.urlopen(urllib.request.Request(url, headers={'User-Agent':'TaiwanStockMaster/1.0','Accept':'application/json,text/csv'}), timeout=25).read().decode('utf-8-sig')\n        except Exception as exc: error = exc\n    raise error\ndef save(name, value): (root / name).write_text(json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False))\ndef number(value):\n    try: return float(re.sub('<[^>]*>', '', str(value)).replace(',', '').strip())\n    except ValueError: return None\n\ndef collect_statistics():\n    # MI_INDEX is the session anchor; turnover OpenAPI can lag by a day.\n    target='https://www.twse.com.tw/rwd/zh/afterTrading/MI_INDEX?type=ALLBUT0999&response=json'\n    latest=json.loads(fetch(target))\n    if latest.get('stat')!='OK' or not re.fullmatch(r'\\d{8}',str(latest.get('date',''))): raise ValueError('Latest MI_INDEX session unavailable')\n    date_key=latest['date'];date=dt.datetime.strptime(date_key,'%Y%m%d').date().isoformat()\n    (rawdir/'statistics-latest-session.json').write_text(json.dumps(latest,ensure_ascii=False))\n    failures=[]\n    for attempt in range(3):\n        try:\n            calendar_url='https://www.twse.com.tw/holidaySchedule/holidaySchedule?response=json'\n            dates_url='https://openapi.twse.com.tw/v1/exchangeReport/FMTQIK'\n            calendar=json.loads(fetch(calendar_url));dates=json.loads(fetch(dates_url))\n            last=max(str(row['Date']) for row in dates)\n            normalized=(str(int(last[:3])+1911)+last[3:]) if len(last)==7 else last\n            if normalized!=date_key:\n                dates_url='https://www.twse.com.tw/exchangeReport/FMTQIK?date='+date_key[:6]+'01&response=json'\n                dates=json.loads(fetch(dates_url))\n                if dates.get('stat')!='OK' or dates.get('fields',[])[0:3:2]!=['日期','成交金額']: raise ValueError('Official FMTQIK monthly schema mismatch')\n                last=max(str(row[0]) for row in dates['data']);parts=last.split('/')\n sources={'calendar':calendar_url,'tradingDates':dates_url,'margin':base+'MI_MARGN?response=json&date='+date_key+'&selectType=MS','marginTrend':base+'MI_MARGN_TREND?response=json&date='+date_key+'&days=30','creditSummary':base+'BFIJ3U?response=json&date='+date_key}\n key in ['margin','marginTrend','creditSummary']:\n                raw=fetch(sources[key]);(rawdir/(key+'-'+date+'.json')).write_text(raw);payload=json.loads(raw)\n                if stats['capturedAt']=now();save('statistics.json',{'schema_version':1,'marketStatistics':stats});return stats\n        except Exception as exc:\n            failures.append(str(exc))\n            if attempt<2: time.sleep(10)\n    raise ValueError('Official statistics incomplete after 3 attempts: '+'; '.join(failures))\n\ndef core_cpi_history(points):\n    points=sorted(points,key=lambda row:row['date'])\n    if len(points)<4 or len({row['date'] for row in points})!=len(points): raise ValueError('Insufficient or duplicate core CPI history')\n    last=points[-4:]\n    month=lambda row:int(row['date'][:4])*12+int(row['date'][5:7])\n    if any(month(b)-month(a)!=1 for a,b in zip(last,last[1:])): raise ValueError('Non-consecutive core CPI history')\n    return points[-24:]\n\ndef fred_url='https://fred.stlouisfed.org/graph/fredgraph.csv?'+urllib.parse.urlencode({'id':'CPILFESL','cosd':(dt.date.today()-dt.timedelta(days=800)).isoformat()})\n    for family,url in [('BLS',bls_url),('FRED',fred_url)]:\n        try:\n            points=[]\n            if family=='BLS':\n                # AllItems omits SA0L1E. Stream Current; never retain 49 MB in memory.\n                try:\n                    request=urllib.request.Request(url,headers={'User-Agent':'Mozilla/5.0','Accept':'text/plain,*/*;q=0.8'})\n                    with urllib.request.urlopen(request,timeout=30) as response, (rawdir/'BLS-core-CPI-current.txt').open('wb') as raw:\n                        columns=None;size=0\n                        for line in response:\n                            size+=len(line)\n                            if size>80_000_000 or len(line)>4096: raise ValueError('BLS CPI file exceeds expected size')\n                            raw.write(line);cells=line.decode('utf-8-sig').split()\n                            if not cells: continue\n                            if columns is None:\n                                columns=[cells.index(key) for key in ['series_id','year','period','value']]\n                                continue\n                            si,yi,pi,vi=columns\n                            if len(cells)<=max(columns) or cells[si]!='CUSR0000SA0L1E' or not re.fullmatch(r'M(?:0[1-9]|1[0-2])',cells[pi]): continue\n                            date=dt.date(int(cells[yi]),int(cells[pi][1:]),1).isoformat();value=number(cells[vi])\n                            if value is not None and value>0 and date<=dt.date.today().isoformat(): points.append({'date':date,'value':value})\n                    core_cpi_history(points)\n                except Exception as file_error:\n                    # The keyless official API is a second BLS route, never an\n                    # independent vote.                        with urllib.request.urlopen(request,timeout=20) as response: raw=response.read(2_000_001)\n                        if len(raw)>2_000_000: raise ValueError('BLS API response exceeds expected size')\n                        (rawdir/'BLS-core-CPI-api.json').write_bytes(raw);body=json.loads(raw)\n                        result=body.get('Results',{}).get('series',[])\n errors.append({'source':'BLS-core-CPI','error':('BLS Current: '+str(file_error)+'; official API fallback used')[:500]})\n            else:\n                raw=fetch(url);(rawdir/'FRED-core-CPI.csv').write_text(raw)\n                for row in csv.DictReader(io.StringIO(raw)):\n even an empty result, so reusing a folder cannot import an old success.\n    save('cpi.json',{'schema_version':1,'coreCpiSources':sources,'coreCpiFailures':errors})\n    same_month=len(sources)==2 and status={'sources':[{'family':row['sourceFamily'],'date':row['history'][-1]['date'],'value':row['history'][-1]['value'],'fetchedAt':row['fetchedAt']} for row in   start=(dt.date.today()-dt.timedelta(days=145 if key=='DEXJPUS' else 45)).isoformat()\n (rawdir/(key+'-release.html')).write_text(page)\n                latest=re.search(r'(\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2}):\\s*<span[^>]*class=\"series-meta-observation-value\"[^>]*>([\\d.,]+)</span>',page)\n +const TIINGO_MARKET=(()=>{/** Private-owner EOD adapter. Never send raw histories to a public relay or logs. */
+const SECTORS=Object.freeze(['XLB','XLC','XLE','XLF','XLI','XLK','XLP','XLRE','XLU','XLV','XLY']);
+const GLOBAL=Object.freeze(['SPY','EWJ','MCHI','EWU','EWC','INDA','EWT','EWG','EWQ','EWY']);
+const SYMBOLS=Object.freeze([...new Set([...SECTORS,'SPY','RSP',...GLOBAL])]);
+const PRICE_FIELD='adjClose';
+const PROVIDER='tiingo-eod-private-v1';
+const DAY=86400000;
+const safeDate=s=>typeof s==='string'&&/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(s)&&Number.isFinite(Date.parse(s))&&new Date(s).toISOString().slice(0,10)===s;
+const goodHistory=h=>Array.isArray(h)&&h.length>0&&h.every((r,i)=>safeDate(r?.date)&&Number.isFinite(r.price)&&r.price>0&&(!i||r.date>h[i-1].date));
+async function fetchTiingoDaily(symbol,{token,expectedSession,fetchImpl=fetch,now=new Date()}={}) {
+  if(!token)return {status:'source_failed',code:'not_configured'};
+  if(!SYMBOLS.includes(symbol))return {status:'source_failed',code:'invalid_symbol'};
+  if(!safeDate(expectedSession))return {status:'source_failed',code:'invalid_expected_session'};
+  const start=new Date(new Date(now).getTime()-430*DAY).toISOString().slice(0,10);
+  const url=`https://api.tiingo.com/tiingo/daily/${symbol}/prices?startDate=${start}&endDate=${expectedSession}&format=json`;
+  let response;
+  try{response=await fetchImpl(url,{headers:{Authorization:`Token ${token}`,Accept:'application/json'},signal:AbortSignal.timeout(12000)});}catch{return {status:'source_failed',code:'network_error'};}
+  if([401,403,429].includes(response.status))return {status:'source_failed',code:response.status===429?'rate_limited':'unauthorized'};
+  if(!response.ok)return {status:'source_failed',code:'upstream_http_error'};
+  let json;try{json=await response.json();}catch{return {status:'source_failed',code:'invalid_json'};}
+  if(!Array.isArray(json)||!json.length)return {status:'source_failed',code:'empty_data'};
+  const unique=new Map();let excludedFutureRows=0;
+  for(const row of json){
+    const date=typeof row?.date==='string'?row.date.slice(0,10):'';
+    // Filter BEFORE determining observedAt: an intraday/next-session bar cannot invalidate an available completed session.
+    if(safeDate(date)&&date>expectedSession){excludedFutureRows++;continue;}
+    if(!safeDate(date)||date<start||!Number.isFinite(row?.adjClose)||row.adjClose<=0)continue;
+    if(unique.has(date)&&unique.get(date).price!==row.adjClose)return {status:'source_failed',code:'conflicting_duplicate'};
+    unique.set(date,{date,price:row.adjClose});
+  }
+  const history=[...unique.values()].sort((a,b)=>a.date.localeCompare(b.date));
+  return history.length?{status:'ok',symbol,history,observedAt:history.at(-1).date,priceField:PRICE_FIELD,excludedFutureRows}:{status:'source_failed',code:'invalid_prices'};
+}
+function selectSession(result,expectedSession,previous=null){
+  const old=previous?.status==='ok'?previous:previous?.previous||null;
+  const failed=code=>({status:'source_failed',reasonCode:code,source:'tiingo',previous:old});
+  if(!safeDate(expectedSession))return failed('invalid_expected_session');
+  if(result?.status!=='ok')return failed(result?.code||'invalid_response');
+  // Defensive second check for stored/imported adapter results as well as HTTP results.
+  const history=result.history?.filter(r=>r.date<=expectedSession);
+  if(!goodHistory(history))return failed('invalid_prices');
+  const observedAt=history.at(-1).date;
+  if(observedAt!==expectedSession)return {...failed('expected_session_missing'),receivedSession:observedAt};
+  return {...result,history,observedAt,status:'ok',source:'tiingo',priceField:PRICE_FIELD};
+}
+function sma200(history){return goodHistory(history)&&history.length>=200?history.slice(-200).reduce((s,r)=>s+r.price,0)/200:null;}
+function breadth(snapshots){
+  const get=s=>snapshots[s]?.status==='ok'&&goodHistory(snapshots[s].history)?snapshots[s].history:null;
+  const count=symbols=>{let above=0;const missing=[];for(const s of symbols){const h=get(s),ma=sma200(h);if(ma===null)missing.push(s);else if(h.at(-1).price>ma)above++;}return {above,coverage:symbols.length-missing.length,total:symbols.length,missing};};
+  const sectors=count(SECTORS),global=count(GLOBAL),spy=get('SPY'),rsp=get('RSP');
+  let equalWeightRatio20d=null,ratioObservedAt=null;
+  if(spy&&rsp){
+    // Require identical last 21 sessions; an intersected gap would silently change the time window.
+    const a=rsp.slice(-21),b=spy.slice(-21);
+    if(a.length===21&&b.length===21&&a.every((r,i)=>r.date===b[i].date)){
+      equalWeightRatio20d=((a.at(-1).price/b.at(-1).price)/(a[0].price/b[0].price)-1)*100;ratioObservedAt=b.at(-1).date;
+    }
+  }
+  const spyAvailable=sma200(spy)!==null;
+  return {priceField:PRICE_FIELD,sectorBreadth:{...sectors,complete:sectors.coverage===11},globalBreadth:{...global,spyAvailable,ratio:spyAvailable&&global.coverage?global.above/global.coverage:null,denominator:spyAvailable?global.coverage:null,complete:global.coverage===10},equalWeightRatio20d,ratioObservedAt,ratioDirection:equalWeightRatio20d===null?'missing':equalWeightRatio20d>0?'rising':equalWeightRatio20d<0?'falling':'flat'};
+}
+async function refreshPrivateMarket({token,expectedSession,cache,quota,now=new Date(),fetchImpl=fetch}){
+  if(!cache?.get||!cache?.set||!quota?.reserve)throw Error('PRIVATE_PERSISTENT_CACHE_AND_ATOMIC_QUOTA_REQUIRED');
+  const snapshots={};let requests=0,stopCode=null;

+const PANEL_SOURCES=(()=>{/** Official lending-sold data. No substituted margin-short or total-lending balances. */
+const TPEX_LENDING_URL='https://www.tpex.org.tw/openapi/v1/tpex_margin_sbl';
+const TWSE_LENDING_BASE='https://www.twse.com.tw/rwd/zh/marginTrading/TWT93U';

+function officialDay(raw){const s=String(raw||'').trim();const date=/^\d{7}$/.test(s)?`${Number(s.slice(0,3))+1911}-${s.slice(3,5)}-${s.slice(5)}`:/^\d{8}$/.test(s)?`${s.slice(0,4)}-${s.slice(4,6)}-${s.slice(6)}`:s;return day(date)?date:null;}

+    for(const r of payload.data||[]){const symbol=String(r[0]),balanceShares=quantity(r[12]),previousShares=quantity(r[8]);if(/^\d{4,6}$/.test(symbol)&&balanceShares!==null)records[symbol]={symbol,market,date:asOf,balanceShares,previousShares,soldShares:quantity(r[9]),returnedShares:quantity(r[10]),adjustedShares:quantity(r[11]),unit:'shares',capturedAt,sourceUrl};}
+  }else if(market==='TPEx'){

+    for(const r of payload){const symbol=String(r.SecuritiesCompanyCode||''),date=officialDay(r.Date),balanceShares=quantity(r.SecuritiesBorrowingBalanceOfTheMarketDay);if(/^\d{4,6}$/.test(symbol)&&date&&date<=asOf&&balanceShares!==null)records[symbol]={symbol,market,date,balanceShares,previousShares:quantity(r.SecuritiesBorrowingBalancePreviousDay),soldShares:quantity(r.SecuritiesBorrowingSale),returnedShares:quantity(r.SecuritiesBorrowingReturn),adjustedShares:quantity(r.SecuritiesBorrowingAdjustment),unit:'shares',capturedAt,sourceUrl};}

+    const results=await Promise.all(urls.map(async([market,sourceUrl])=>{

+const ADVISORY_DECISION=(()=>{/** Deterministic research preview. All proposed points are UNAPPROVED and have zero active contribution. */
+const finite=x=>typeof x==='number'&&Number.isFinite(x);
+const validRow=(r,asOf)=>r&&['ok','snapshot'].includes(r.status)&&r.is_stale!==true&&finite(r.value)&&r.date<=asOf;
+const avg=a=>a.reduce((s,x)=>s+x,0)/a.length;
+const factor=(id,label,value,points,rule,extra={})=>({id,label,value,draftPoints:points,rule,approved:false,activeContribution:0,status:points===null?'pending':'draft',...extra});
+function changes(row,sessions,asOf){
+ if(!validRow(row,asOf))return {status:'missing',reason:'SOURCE_MISSING_STALE_OR_FUTURE'};
+ const points=(row.history||[]).filter(r=>r.date<=asOf&&finite(r.value)).sort((a,b)=>a.date.localeCompare(b.date));
+ if(points.length<sessions+1||points.at(-1).date!==row.date||new Set(points.map(r=>r.date)).size!==points.length)return {status:'insufficient',reason:`REQUIRE_${sessions+1}_DISTINCT_ALIGNED_OBSERVATIONS`};
+ return {status:'ok',date:row.date,value:points.at(-1).value-points.at(-sessions-1).value,unit:row.unit,source:row.source,observations:points.length};
+}
+function attackDefense(context){
+ const attack=['XLK','XLY','XLC','XLI','XLF','XLE','XLB'],defense=['XLP','XLU','XLV','XLRE'];
+ const get=s=>context?.sectors?.[s]?.status==='OK'&&context.sectors[s].observedAt===context.observedAt&&finite(context.sectors[s].relative?.return20d)?context.sectors[s].relative.return20d:null;
+ const a=attack.map(get),b=defense.map(get);

+}
+function marketAnnualLine(snapshot,asOf){
+ const entry=snapshot?.markets?.TWSE_TAIEX,rows=(entry?.history||[]).filter(r=>r.date<=asOf&&finite(r.value)&&r.value>0).sort((a,b)=>a.date.localeCompare(b.date));
+ if(rows.length<241||new Set(rows.map(r=>r.date)).size!==rows.length)return {status:'insufficient',reason:'REQUIRE_241_DISTINCT_TAIEX_SESSIONS',observations:rows.length};

+ const globalFactors=[
+  factor('dxy','美元零軸',axis.value,axis.status==='ok'?(axis.value>0?-1:axis.value<-100?1:0):null,'草案：>0 −1；<−100 +1；其間0',{date:axis.date,source:axis.source,reason:axis.reason}),

+ const stockFactors=[
+  factor('moving_averages','均線強弱',t.score,t.status==='ok'?t.score:null,'草案：四條均線各−1/0/+1，合計−4～+4',{date:t.date}),

+  factor('eps_high','季EPS觀測區間新高',e.newHigh,null,'新高已計算；加分權重待核，與EPS surprise分開',{date:e.date,reason:'WEIGHT_PENDING'}),
