```diff
diff --git a/tests/advisory-decision.test.mjs b/tests/advisory-decision.test.mjs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..1f3dbe7
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/advisory-decision.test.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,15 @@
+import test from 'node:test';
+import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
+import {readFileSync} from 'node:fs';
+import {buildAdvisoryDecision,previewHoldingRange,attackDefense,marketAnnualLine} from '../worker/advisory-decision.mjs';
+import {buildStockPanels} from '../worker/panel-calculations.mjs';
+const stock={id:'7788',price:{date:'2026-10-19'},priceHistory:[],financialQuality:{cfoToNetIncome:{value:-.17},freeCashFlow:{value:-141.57e8}}};
+const args=()=>({stock:structuredClone(stock),panels:buildStockPanels(stock),existingRating:{available:true,grade:'D'},veto:{status:'unknown'},gates:[{id:6,status:'warning'}]});
+test('same facts give byte-identical deterministic conclusion; no input mutation',()=>{const a=args(),before=JSON.stringify(a),x=buildAdvisoryDecision(a),y=buildAdvisoryDecision(a);assert.deepEqual(x,y);assert.equal(JSON.stringify(a),before);assert.match(x.conclusion,/保守觀察/);assert.match(x.reasons[0],/−?\-0\.17/);assert.equal(x.reasons.length,3);assert.equal(x.originalGateWarnings[0].id,6);});
+test('missing or unapproved factors never count as zero, total and allocation stay unset',()=>{const r=buildAdvisoryDecision(args());assert.equal(r.globalSummary.total,null);assert.ok(r.globalSummary.known<r.globalSummary.required);assert.equal(r.holding.suggestedRange,null);assert.deepEqual(r.holding.baseRange,[60,70]);assert.deepEqual(r.holding.floorRange,[20,30]);assert.ok(r.gaps.length);});
+test('gold cannot change a factor, active rating contribution or risk budget',()=>{const a=args(),before=buildAdvisoryDecision(a);a.panels.goldZeroAxis={status:'ok',value:9999};const after=buildAdvisoryDecision(a);assert.deepEqual(after.globalSummary,before.globalSummary);assert.equal(after.activeScoreContribution,0);assert.equal(after.appliedToRating,false);assert.equal(after.appliedToRiskBudget,false);assert.equal(after.gold.activeContribution,0);assert.ok(after.globalFactors.every(f=>f.activeContribution===0&&!f.approved));});
+test('an existing VETO always takes precedence over positive research previews',()=>{const a=args();a.veto={status:'triggered'};a.panels.movingAverages={status:'ok',score:4};assert.match(buildAdvisoryDecision(a).conclusion,/剔除/);});
+test('holding mapping requires an explicit step, simulation never applies a budget',()=>{assert.equal(previewHoldingRange({score:-2}).suggestedRange,null);const sim=previewHoldingRange({score:-20,stepPp:5});assert.deepEqual(sim.suggestedRange,[20,30]);assert.equal(sim.applied,false);assert.deepEqual(previewHoldingRange({score:10,stepPp:5}).suggestedRange,[60,70]);});
+test('attack-defense zero axis uses all aligned eleven sectors',()=>{const sectors=Object.fromEntries(['XLK','XLY','XLC','XLI','XLF','XLE','XLB','XLP','XLU','XLV','XLRE'].map((s,i)=>[s,{status:'OK',observedAt:'2026-10-16',relative:{return20d:i<7?3:1}}]));assert.equal(attackDefense({sectors,observedAt:'2026-10-16'}).value,2);sectors.XLK.observedAt='2026-10-15';assert.equal(attackDefense({sectors,observedAt:'2026-10-16'}).status,'insufficient');});
+test('TAIEX annual line cannot be invented from the existing short history',()=>{assert.equal(marketAnnualLine({markets:{TWSE_TAIEX:{history:[]}}},'2026-10-19').draftPoints,undefined);});
+test('real Site report adds advisory conclusion without changing original scores or gates',async()=>{const s=readFileSync(new URL('../dist/server/index.js',import.meta.url),'utf8');const api=await import('data:text/javascript;base64,'+Buffer.from(s+'\nexport {evaluateMasterModel};').toString('base64'));const fixture=JSON.parse(readFileSync(new URL('./fixtures/master-remediation.json',import.meta.url),'utf8')).stock;const model=api.evaluateMasterModel(fixture,null,null,{stockFresh:true},{});assert.equal(model.report.advisoryDecision.appliedToRiskBudget,false);assert.equal(model.report.advisoryDecision.appliedToRating,false);assert.equal(model.report.advisoryDecision.stockFactors[0].activeContribution,0);assert.ok(model.gates.some(g=>g.id===6&&g.status==='warning'));});
diff --git a/tests/build-reproducibility.test.mjs b/tests/build-reproducibility.test.mjs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..40352db
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/build-reproducibility.test.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,10 @@
+import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
+import {execFileSync} from 'node:child_process';
+import {readFileSync} from 'node:fs';
+import {fileURLToPath} from 'node:url';
+const cwd=fileURLToPath(new URL('..',import.meta.url)),paths=['dist/index.html','dist/server/index.js'];
+execFileSync(process.execPath,['build.mjs'],{cwd});
+const first=paths.map(p=>readFileSync(new URL('../'+p,import.meta.url),'utf8'));
+execFileSync(process.execPath,['build.mjs'],{cwd});
+for(let i=0;i<paths.length;i++)assert.equal(readFileSync(new URL('../'+paths[i],import.meta.url),'utf8'),first[i],paths[i]+' must not change when rebuilt');
+console.log('build reproducibility: two successive builds preserve byte-identical HTML and Worker');
diff --git a/tests/panel-calculations.test.mjs b/tests/panel-calculations.test.mjs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..3f4484f
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/panel-calculations.test.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,41 @@
+import test from 'node:test';
+import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
+import {movingAverageStrength,zeroAxis,revenueHigh,quarterEpsHigh,priceBookBand,mapIndustry,buildStockPanels} from '../worker/panel-calculations.mjs';
+const prices=Array.from({length:65},(_,i)=>({date:new Date(Date.parse('2026-08-01')+i*86400000).toISOString().slice(0,10),close:100+i}));
+test('MA score requires price position AND slope, all four windows; flat=0, missing is not zero',()=>{
+ assert.equal(movingAverageStrength(prices).score,4);assert.equal(movingAverageStrength(prices.map((r,i)=>({...r,close:200-i}))).score,-4);assert.equal(movingAverageStrength(prices.map(r=>({...r,close:100}))).score,0);
+ assert.equal(movingAverageStrength(prices.slice(-60)).score,null);assert.equal(movingAverageStrength(prices,{aligned:false}).score,null);assert.equal(movingAverageStrength(prices,{asOf:'2026-10-10'}).score,null);
+});
+test('DXY zero axis excludes today, computes five-session-ago independently and rejects flat range',()=>{
+ const data=Array.from({length:26},(_,i)=>({date:new Date(Date.parse('2026-09-01')+i*86400000).toISOString().slice(0,10),value:i+10}));const r=zeroAxis(data,{id:'ICE_DXY'});
+ assert.ok(Math.abs(r.value-100/19)<1e-10);assert.ok(Math.abs(r.previous5.value-100/19)<1e-10);assert.equal(r.priorHigh,34);
+ data.at(-1).value=15;assert.equal(zeroAxis(data).value,-100);data.at(-1).value=14;assert.ok(zeroAxis(data).value<-100);
+ assert.equal(zeroAxis(data.map(x=>({...x,value:1}))).reason,'FLAT_PRIOR_RANGE');assert.equal(zeroAxis(data.slice(-20)).value,undefined);
+});
+test('monthly records preserve observed-window claim and require three actual year-ago matches',()=>{
+ const rows=Array.from({length:26},(_,i)=>({revenue_year:2024+Math.floor(i/12),revenue_month:i%12+1,revenue:100+i}));let r=revenueHigh(rows);assert.equal(r.newHigh,true);assert.equal(r.threePositiveYoY,true);assert.equal(r.scope,'observed_window_only_not_all_time');
+ rows.splice(12,1);r=revenueHigh(rows);assert.equal(r.threePositiveYoY,null);
+ assert.equal(revenueHigh(rows.slice(-2)).newHigh,null);
+});
+test('explicit cumulative EPS is differenced by year, single-quarter EPS never differenced twice',()=>{
+ const dates=['2024-03-31','2024-06-30','2024-09-30','2024-12-31','2025-03-31'];const rows=dates.map((date,i)=>({date,value:[1,3,6,10,5][i]}));
+ const r=quarterEpsHigh(rows,{basis:'ytd'});assert.equal(r.newHigh,true);assert.equal(r.priorHigh,4);
+ assert.equal(quarterEpsHigh(rows,{basis:'single_quarter'}).newHigh,false);assert.equal(quarterEpsHigh(rows).reason,'EPS_PERIOD_BASIS_UNKNOWN');
+});
+test('PBR does not depend on positive PER, missing markers do not become zero, current must match date',()=>{
+ const rows=Array.from({length:260},(_,i)=>({date:new Date(Date.parse('2025-01-01')+i*86400000).toISOString().slice(0,10),PER:-1,PBR:1+i/1000}));const date=rows.at(-1).date;
+ assert.equal(priceBookBand(rows,{asOf:date}).status,'ok');assert.equal(priceBookBand(rows,{asOf:'2026-10-19'}).reason,'SAME_DAY_PBR_MISSING');assert.equal(priceBookBand(rows.map(x=>({...x,PBR:'-'})),{asOf:date}).observations,0);
+});
+const issuer=(code,extra={})=>({status:'official',symbol:'7788',industryCode:code,date:'2026-10-19',market:'TWSE',sourceUrl:'https://openapi.twse.com.tw/v1/opendata/t187ap03_L',...extra});
+test('official industry mapping follows the current ticker metadata, not a hardcoded watchlist',()=>{
+ assert.equal(mapIndustry(issuer('24')).sector,'XLK');assert.equal(mapIndustry(issuer('10')).sector,'XLB');assert.equal(mapIndustry(issuer('17')).sector,'XLF');assert.equal(mapIndustry(issuer('18')).sector,'XLY');
+ assert.equal(mapIndustry(issuer('31')).sector,null);assert.equal(mapIndustry(issuer('14')).sector,null);assert.equal(mapIndustry(issuer('19')).sector,null);assert.equal(mapIndustry(issuer('20')).sector,null);
+ const businessEvidence={status:'verified',date:'2026-05-27',text:'無塵室廠務工程承攬',sourceUrl:'https://doc.twse.com.tw/pdf/official-annual.pdf'};assert.equal(mapIndustry(issuer('31',{businessEvidence})).sector,'XLI');
+ assert.equal(mapIndustry(issuer('24',{date:'2026-10-21'}),{asOf:'2026-10-20'}).sector,null);assert.equal(mapIndustry(issuer('24',{status:'user_guess'})).sector,null);
+});
+test('stale macro values and substituted broad dollar index never produce a DXY axis',()=>{
+ const stock={id:'7788',price:{date:'2026-10-19'},priceHistory:prices,panelInputs:{issuer:issuer('24')}};
+ assert.equal(buildStockPanels(stock,[{id:'DTWEXBGS',date:'2026-10-19',status:'ok',history:[]}]).dollarZeroAxis.status,'insufficient');
+ assert.equal(buildStockPanels(stock,[{id:'ICE_DXY',date:'2026-10-19',status:'stale',history:[]}]).dollarZeroAxis.status,'insufficient');
+ assert.equal(buildStockPanels(stock).activeScoreContribution,0);assert.equal(buildStockPanels(stock).goldZeroAxis.activeScoreContribution,0);
+});
diff --git a/tests/panel-display.test.mjs b/tests/panel-display.test.mjs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..415a897
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/panel-display.test.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,31 @@
+import test from 'node:test';
+import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
+import {readFileSync} from 'node:fs';
+import vm from 'node:vm';
+import {buildStockPanels} from '../worker/panel-calculations.mjs';
+class Element{
+ constructor(tag='div'){this.tagName=tag;this.children=[];this.style={};this.attributes={};this.hidden=false;}
+ append(...items){this.children.push(...items);}
+ replaceChildren(...items){this.children=items;this.text='';}
+ set textContent(v){this.text=String(v);this.children=[];}
+ get textContent(){return(this.text||'')+this.children.map(x=>x.textContent||String(x)).join(' ');}
+ setAttribute(k,v){this.attributes[k]=v;}
+}
+function setup(){const host=new Element(),context=vm.createContext({document:{getElementById:()=>host,createElement:tag=>new Element(tag)},console});vm.runInContext(readFileSync(new URL('../worker/panel-display.js',import.meta.url),'utf8'),context);return {host,context};}
+test('new metrics display missing facts honestly and clear immediately on ticker change',()=>{
+ const {host,context}=setup(),stock={id:'7788',price:{date:'2026-10-19'},priceHistory:[],panelInputs:{}};
+ context.model={symbol:'7788',report:{advisoryPanels:buildStockPanels(stock)}};vm.runInContext('renderAdvisoryPanels(model)',context);
+ assert.equal(host.hidden,false);for(const label of ['均線強弱','月營收創高','季 EPS 創高','借券賣出餘額','股價淨值比','美元指數零軸','自動對應美股板塊','對應不明','草案','不改既有總分'])assert.ok(host.textContent.includes(label),label);
+ vm.runInContext('renderAdvisoryPanels(null)',context);assert.equal(host.hidden,true);assert.equal(host.children.length,0);
+});
+test('an extreme zero-axis observation cannot freeze the dashboard by creating unbounded ticks',()=>{
+ const {host,context}=setup();context.host=host;vm.runInContext("advisoryAxis(host,{status:'ok',value:100000000},'DXY')",context);const chart=host.children[0];assert.ok(chart.children.length<=86);assert.equal(chart.attributes.role,'img');
+});
+test('real bundled model exposes new panels while preserving existing rating and Gate outputs',async()=>{
+ const source=readFileSync(new URL('../dist/server/index.js',import.meta.url),'utf8');const api=await import('data:text/javascript;base64,'+Buffer.from(source+'\nexport {evaluateMasterModel};').toString('base64'));
+ const stock=JSON.parse(readFileSync(new URL('./fixtures/master-remediation.json',import.meta.url),'utf8')).stock;
+ const a=api.evaluateMasterModel(structuredClone(stock),null,null,{},{});
+ const b=api.evaluateMasterModel({...structuredClone(stock),panelInputs:{issuer:{symbol:stock.id,status:'official',industryCode:'24',date:stock.price.date,sourceUrl:'https://www.tpex.org.tw/openapi/v1/mopsfin_t187ap03_O',market:'TPEx'},epsBasis:'single_quarter'}},null,null,{},{});
+ assert.equal(b.report.advisoryPanels.symbol,stock.id);assert.equal(b.report.advisoryPanels.industryMapping.sector,'XLK');
+ assert.deepEqual(b.gates.map(g=>g.status),a.gates.map(g=>g.status));assert.deepEqual(b.scoring,a.scoring);assert.deepEqual(b.veto,a.veto);assert.deepEqual(b.adaptiveRating,a.adaptiveRating);
+});
diff --git a/tests/panel-sources.test.mjs b/tests/panel-sources.test.mjs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..1eae96b
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/panel-sources.test.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,27 @@
+import test from 'node:test';
+import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
+import {parseLending,selectAndSaveLending,collectLending,TPEX_LENDING_URL,TWSE_LENDING_BASE} from '../worker/panel-sources.mjs';
+const now=Date.parse('2026-10-20T12:00:00Z'),asOf='2026-10-19',capturedAt=new Date(now).toISOString();
+const twse={stat:'OK',date:'20261019',hints:'單位：股',fields:['代號','名稱','前日餘額','賣出','買進','現券','今日餘額','次一營業日限額','前日餘額','當日賣出','當日還券','當日調整','當日餘額','次一營業日可限額','備註'],groups:[{title:'股票',span:2},{title:'融券',span:6},{title:'借券賣出',span:6}],data:[['7788','測試公司','2','0','0','0','2','999','100','20','10','0','110','200','']]};
+const tpex=[{Date:'1151019',SecuritiesCompanyCode:'7799',SecuritiesBorrowingBalanceOfTheMarketDay:'220',SecuritiesBorrowingBalancePreviousDay:'200',SaleBalanceOfTheMarketDay:'3'}];
+const opts={capturedAt,asOf,sourceUrl:TWSE_LENDING_BASE};
+test('parse distinct TWSE/TPEx lending-sold balance columns, correct share units and ROC date',()=>{
+ const a=parseLending(twse,'TWSE',opts)['7788'];assert.equal(a.balanceShares,110);assert.equal(a.previousShares,100);assert.equal(a.unit,'shares');
+ const b=parseLending(tpex,'TPEx',{...opts,sourceUrl:TPEX_LENDING_URL})['7799'];assert.equal(b.balanceShares,220);assert.equal(b.date,asOf);
+ assert.throws(()=>parseLending({...twse,groups:[]},'TWSE',opts));assert.throws(()=>parseLending({...twse,hints:'單位：張'},'TWSE',opts));
+});
+test('future observations and unknown schema are not accepted',()=>{
+ assert.throws(()=>parseLending({...twse,date:'20261020'},'TWSE',opts));assert.throws(()=>parseLending([{...tpex[0],Date:'1151020'}],'TPEx',opts));
+ assert.throws(()=>parseLending([{Date:'1151019',SecuritiesCompanyCode:'7799',SaleBalanceOfTheMarketDay:'20'}],'TPEx',opts));
+});
+test('ratio requires same-day shares, original observation saved; no 5-day trend invented from one point',async()=>{
+ const values=new Map(),read=async k=>({payload:values.get(k)}),write=async(k,v)=>{values.set(k,v);return true;},pool={stocks:parseLending(twse,'TWSE',opts)};
+ let r=await selectAndSaveLending({pool,symbol:'7788',market:'TWSE',asOf,issuer:{date:'2026-10-16',shares:1000},read,write});assert.equal(r.balanceRatioPct,null);assert.equal(r.deltas[5],null);assert.equal(r.history.length,1);
+ r=await selectAndSaveLending({pool,symbol:'7788',market:'TWSE',asOf,issuer:{date:asOf,shares:1000,sourceUrl:'official'},read,write});assert.equal(r.balanceRatioPct,11);assert.equal(r.history.length,1);assert.equal(r.capturedAt,capturedAt);
+ r=await selectAndSaveLending({pool,symbol:'7788',market:'TPEx',asOf,read,write});assert.equal(r.status,'insufficient');
+});
+test('shared dated cache fetches both markets once; one official failure remains visible',async()=>{
+ const map=new Map(),read=async k=>map.has(k)?{payload:map.get(k),fresh:true}:null,write=async(k,v)=>{map.set(k,v);return true;};let calls=0;
+ const fetchImpl=async url=>{calls++;return url.includes('/TWT93U')?Response.json(twse):new Response('',{status:503});};
+ const a=await collectLending({asOf,read,write,fetchImpl,now}),b=await collectLending({asOf,read,write,fetchImpl,now});assert.equal(calls,2);assert.equal(a.status,'partial');assert.equal(b.errors[0].market,'TPEx');assert.equal(b.stocks['7788'].balanceShares,110);assert.equal(b.stocks['7799'],undefined);
+});
diff --git a/tests/tiingo-market.test.mjs b/tests/tiingo-market.test.mjs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..cc8768b
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/tiingo-market.test.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,67 @@
+import test from 'node:test';
+import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
+import {readFileSync} from 'node:fs';
+import {spawnSync} from 'node:child_process';
+import {SECTORS,GLOBAL,SYMBOLS,fetchTiingoDaily,selectSession,breadth,refreshPrivateMarket} from '../worker/tiingo-market.mjs';
+import {GUARD_SCHEMA,RESERVE_SQL,LEASE_SQL} from '../worker/tiingo-store.mjs';
+const now=new Date('2026-10-20T12:00:00Z'),expectedSession='2026-10-19';
+const history=Array.from({length:270},(_,i)=>({date:new Date(Date.parse(expectedSession)-(269-i)*86400000).toISOString().slice(0,10),price:100+i}));
+const item=(symbol,h=history)=>({status:'ok',symbol,priceField:'adjClose',observedAt:h.at(-1).date,history:h});
+const snapshots=()=>Object.fromEntries(SYMBOLS.map(s=>[s,item(s)]));
+
+test('exact 22 tickers; ten markets, one vote each',()=>{assert.equal(SYMBOLS.length,22);assert.equal(SECTORS.length,11);assert.deepEqual(GLOBAL,['SPY','EWJ','MCHI','EWU','EWC','INDA','EWT','EWG','EWQ','EWY']);});
+test('exclude later-than-expected bars before observedAt; auth header only, adjClose only',async()=>{
+  let call;const response=[{date:'2026-10-16',adjClose:101,close:1},{date:'2026-10-19',adjClose:102,close:2},{date:'2026-10-20',adjClose:9999,close:3}];
+  const r=await fetchTiingoDaily('SPY',{token:'synthetic-test-credential',expectedSession,now,fetchImpl:async(u,o)=>{call={u,o};return Response.json(response);}});
+  assert.equal(r.observedAt,expectedSession);assert.equal(r.history.at(-1).price,102);assert.equal(r.excludedFutureRows,1);assert.equal(selectSession(r,expectedSession).status,'ok');
+  assert.equal(call.o.headers.Authorization,'Token synthetic-test-credential');assert.ok(!call.u.includes('synthetic-test-credential'));assert.ok(!JSON.stringify(r).includes('credential'));
+});
+test('defensive stored-result filtering and missing completed session retain original last-good',()=>{
+  const old=item('SPY',history.slice(0,-1)),future={...item('SPY'),history:[...history,{date:'2026-10-20',price:999}]};
+  assert.equal(selectSession(future,expectedSession,old).observedAt,expectedSession);
+  const failed=selectSession(old,expectedSession,old);assert.equal(failed.status,'source_failed');assert.equal(failed.previous.observedAt,'2026-10-18');
+});
+test('401/403/429/empty/invalid JSON/exception are sanitized',async()=>{
+  for(const status of [401,403,429,503]){const r=await fetchTiingoDaily('SPY',{token:'test-secret',expectedSession,now,fetchImpl:async()=>new Response('test-secret',{status})});assert.equal(r.status,'source_failed');assert.ok(!JSON.stringify(r).includes('test-secret'));}
+  for(const fetchImpl of [async()=>Response.json([]),async()=>new Response('broken'),async()=>{throw Error('test-secret');}])assert.equal((await fetchTiingoDaily('SPY',{token:'test-secret',expectedSession,now,fetchImpl})).status,'source_failed');
+});
+test('conflicting duplicates are rejected, not overwritten',async()=>{const r=await fetchTiingoDaily('SPY',{token:'test',expectedSession,now,fetchImpl:async()=>Response.json([{date:expectedSession,adjClose:1},{date:expectedSession,adjClose:2}])});assert.equal(r.code,'conflicting_duplicate');});
+test('200 samples required; partial global breadth uses observed denominator only, SPY mandatory',()=>{
+  const data=snapshots();data.EWJ=item('EWJ',history.slice(-199));let b=breadth(data);assert.equal(b.globalBreadth.coverage,9);assert.equal(b.globalBreadth.denominator,9);assert.equal(b.globalBreadth.ratio,1);assert.equal(b.globalBreadth.complete,false);
+  data.SPY={status:'source_failed'};b=breadth(data);assert.equal(b.globalBreadth.ratio,null);assert.equal(b.globalBreadth.denominator,null);
+  data.XLK=item('XLK',history.slice(-199));assert.equal(breadth(data).sectorBreadth.coverage,10);
+});
+test('RSP/SPY is exactly 20 aligned sessions, not intersection extended across a hole',()=>{const data=snapshots();assert.equal(breadth(data).equalWeightRatio20d,0);data.RSP.history=data.RSP.history.filter((_,i)=>i!==260);assert.equal(breadth(data).equalWeightRatio20d,null);});
+test('persistent same-session cache survives a fresh refresh invocation without quota/fetch',async()=>{
+  const map=new Map(Object.entries(snapshots()).map(([s,x])=>[`tiingo:${s}`,x]));let calls=0;
+  const cache={get:async k=>map.get(k),set:async(k,x)=>{map.set(k,x);return true;}},quota={reserve:async()=>{calls++;return true;}};
+  const r=await refreshPrivateMarket({token:'test',expectedSession,now,cache,quota,fetchImpl:async()=>{throw Error('should not fetch');}});assert.equal(r.requestCount,0);assert.equal(calls,0);
+});
+test('429 stops the fan-out, persists cooldown; next invocation makes zero requests',async()=>{
+  const map=new Map(),cache={get:async k=>map.get(k),set:async(k,x)=>{map.set(k,x);return true;}},quota={reserve:async()=>true};let calls=0;
+  const args={token:'test',expectedSession,now,cache,quota,fetchImpl:async()=>{calls++;return new Response('',{status:429});}};
+  await refreshPrivateMarket(args);await refreshPrivateMarket(args);assert.equal(calls,1);assert.equal(map.size,22);
+});
+test('without persistent cache and atomic reservation no network call is allowed',async()=>{await assert.rejects(refreshPrivateMarket({token:'test',expectedSession,now,cache:{get(){},set(){}},quota:{canFetch(){},record(){}}}),/ATOMIC_QUOTA/);});
+test('actual SQLite: concurrent reservations cap at 50, rolling-hour boundary, day/month guards and single refresh lease',()=>{
+  const code=`import json,sqlite3,tempfile,concurrent.futures,sys,os
+d=json.load(sys.stdin);tmp=tempfile.TemporaryDirectory();path=os.path.join(tmp.name,'quota.db');now=1792497600000
+def conn():
+ c=sqlite3.connect(path,timeout=30);c.execute('PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL');return c
+c=conn();c.execute(d['schema']);c.execute('INSERT INTO tiingo_private_guard (id) VALUES (1)');c.commit();c.close()
+def reserve(i,t=now):
+ c=conn();sym='SPY';month='2026-10';p='$.SPY';args=[t-86400000,json.dumps({'at':t,'symbol':sym}),month,p,sym,month,t-3600000,t-86400000,month,p]
+ r=c.execute(d['reserve'],args).fetchall();c.commit();c.close();return bool(r)
+with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=12) as ex: results=list(ex.map(reserve,range(100)))
+assert sum(results)==50,sum(results)
+assert not reserve(0,now+3599999)
+assert reserve(0,now+3600001)
+c=conn();c.execute('UPDATE tiingo_private_guard SET requests=?',(json.dumps([{'at':now-4000000,'symbol':'SPY'}]*1000),));c.commit();assert not reserve(0)
+c.execute('UPDATE tiingo_private_guard SET requests=?, symbols=?, month=?',('[]',json.dumps({str(i):1 for i in range(500)}),'2026-10'));c.commit();assert not reserve(0)
+c.execute('UPDATE tiingo_private_guard SET symbols=?',('{}',));c.commit()
+def lease(i):
+ c=conn();r=c.execute(d['lease'],[str(i),now+900000,now]).fetchall();c.commit();c.close();return bool(r)
+with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=8) as ex: assert sum(ex.map(lease,range(32)))==1
+print('SQL concurrency and all limits passed')`;
+  const r=spawnSync('python',['-c',code],{input:JSON.stringify({schema:GUARD_SCHEMA,reserve:RESERVE_SQL,lease:LEASE_SQL}),encoding:'utf8'});assert.equal(r.status,0,r.stderr);
+});
diff --git a/tests/tiingo-site.test.mjs b/tests/tiingo-site.test.mjs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..2c93eda
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/tiingo-site.test.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,26 @@
+import test from 'node:test';
+import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
+import {readFileSync} from 'node:fs';
+import {SYMBOLS,PROVIDER} from '../worker/tiingo-market.mjs';
+import {calendarCovers,isSession} from '../worker/us-sector-session.mjs';
+const source=readFileSync(new URL('../dist/server/index.js',import.meta.url),'utf8');
+const api=await import('data:text/javascript;base64,'+Buffer.from(source+'\nexport {normalizeTiingoSnapshot,evaluateUSSectorContext,compareMarketProviders,refreshTiingoSite};').toString('base64'));
+const now=Date.parse('2026-10-20T12:00:00Z'),dates=[];
+for(let t=Date.parse('2025-09-01');t<=Date.parse('2026-10-19');t+=86400000){const day=new Date(t).toISOString().slice(0,10);if(calendarCovers(day)?isSession(day):![0,6].includes(new Date(t).getUTCDay()))dates.push(day);}
+const make=()=>({provider:PROVIDER,expectedSession:'2026-10-19',checkedAt:new Date(now).toISOString(),snapshots:Object.fromEntries(SYMBOLS.map((s,j)=>[s,{symbol:s,status:'ok',priceField:'adjClose',history:dates.map((d,i)=>({date:d,price:100+j+i*(1+j/1000)}))}]))});
+test('Site uses full private adjClose history independently, including prior calendar year',()=>{
+ const p=make(),data=api.normalizeTiingoSnapshot(p,now),r=api.evaluateUSSectorContext(data,{now});
+ assert.equal(r.status,'OK');assert.equal(r.priceField,'adjClose');assert.equal(r.sourceRole,'主要來源');assert.equal(r.coverage.valid,11);
+ const h=p.snapshots.XLK.history;assert.ok(Math.abs(r.sectors.XLK.returns.return20d-(h.at(-1).price/h.at(-21).price-1)*100)<1e-10);
+ assert.equal(r.breadth.sectorBreadth.coverage,11);assert.equal(r.breadth.globalBreadth.coverage,10);assert.equal(r.scoreContribution,0);assert.equal(r.macroGateOverride,null);assert.equal(r.vetoOverride,null);
+ assert.ok(!JSON.stringify(r).includes('"histories"'));assert.ok(!JSON.stringify(r).includes('"snapshots"'));
+});
+test('Taiwan-close cutoff applies to breadth as well as relative returns',()=>{const r=api.evaluateUSSectorContext(api.normalizeTiingoSnapshot(make(),now),{now,referenceDate:'2026-10-19'});assert.equal(r.observedAt,'2026-10-16');assert.equal(r.breadth.ratioObservedAt,'2026-10-16');});
+test('same-date providers compare 33 relative returns; large differences block primary',()=>{
+ const p=api.normalizeTiingoSnapshot(make(),now),y={...structuredClone(p),provider:undefined,source:'Yahoo Finance chart API (unofficial)'};
+ for(const rows of Object.values(y.histories))rows.splice(0,rows.length-66);
+ assert.equal(api.compareMarketProviders(p,y).status,'within_guard');assert.equal(api.compareMarketProviders(p,y).rows.length,33);
+ y.histories.XLK.at(-1).close*=1.5;assert.equal(api.compareMarketProviders(p,y).status,'blocked');
+});
+test('one failed global market is partial; failed SPY makes primary unavailable',()=>{const p=make();p.snapshots.EWJ={status:'source_failed',reasonCode:'rate_limited'};const r=api.evaluateUSSectorContext(api.normalizeTiingoSnapshot(p,now),{now});assert.equal(r.breadth.globalBreadth.coverage,9);assert.equal(r.breadth.globalBreadth.denominator,9);p.snapshots.SPY={status:'source_failed'};assert.throws(()=>api.normalizeTiingoSnapshot(p,now),/BENCHMARK/);});
+test('missing private DB fails closed before any Tiingo request',async()=>{let calls=0;const old=globalThis.fetch;globalThis.fetch=async()=>{calls++;throw Error('unexpected');};try{const r=await api.refreshTiingoSite({TIINGO_API_TOKEN:'test-only'},now);assert.equal(r.fetchError,'PRIVATE_STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE');assert.equal(calls,0);}finally{globalThis.fetch=old;}});
diff --git a/worker/advisory-decision.mjs b/worker/advisory-decision.mjs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..a7a34a4
--- /dev/null
+++ b/worker/advisory-decision.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,65 @@
+/** Deterministic research preview. All proposed points are UNAPPROVED and have zero active contribution. */
+const finite=x=>typeof x==='number'&&Number.isFinite(x);
+const validRow=(r,asOf)=>r&&['ok','snapshot'].includes(r.status)&&r.is_stale!==true&&finite(r.value)&&r.date<=asOf;
+const avg=a=>a.reduce((s,x)=>s+x,0)/a.length;
+const factor=(id,label,value,points,rule,extra={})=>({id,label,value,draftPoints:points,rule,approved:false,activeContribution:0,status:points===null?'pending':'draft',...extra});
+function changes(row,sessions,asOf){
+ if(!validRow(row,asOf))return {status:'missing',reason:'SOURCE_MISSING_STALE_OR_FUTURE'};
+ const points=(row.history||[]).filter(r=>r.date<=asOf&&finite(r.value)).sort((a,b)=>a.date.localeCompare(b.date));
+ if(points.length<sessions+1||points.at(-1).date!==row.date||new Set(points.map(r=>r.date)).size!==points.length)return {status:'insufficient',reason:`REQUIRE_${sessions+1}_DISTINCT_ALIGNED_OBSERVATIONS`};
+ return {status:'ok',date:row.date,value:points.at(-1).value-points.at(-sessions-1).value,unit:row.unit,source:row.source,observations:points.length};
+}
+export function attackDefense(context){
+ const attack=['XLK','XLY','XLC','XLI','XLF','XLE','XLB'],defense=['XLP','XLU','XLV','XLRE'];
+ const get=s=>context?.sectors?.[s]?.status==='OK'&&context.sectors[s].observedAt===context.observedAt&&finite(context.sectors[s].relative?.return20d)?context.sectors[s].relative.return20d:null;
+ const a=attack.map(get),b=defense.map(get);
+ if([...a,...b].some(v=>v===null))return {status:'insufficient',reason:'REQUIRE_11_ALIGNED_SECTORS'};
+ return {status:'ok',date:context.observedAt,value:avg(a)-avg(b),attackAverage:avg(a),defenseAverage:avg(b),source:context.source,priceField:context.priceField,unit:'percentage_points'};
+}
+export function marketAnnualLine(snapshot,asOf){
+ const entry=snapshot?.markets?.TWSE_TAIEX,rows=(entry?.history||[]).filter(r=>r.date<=asOf&&finite(r.value)&&r.value>0).sort((a,b)=>a.date.localeCompare(b.date));
+ if(rows.length<241||new Set(rows.map(r=>r.date)).size!==rows.length)return {status:'insufficient',reason:'REQUIRE_241_DISTINCT_TAIEX_SESSIONS',observations:rows.length};
+ if(rows.at(-1).date!==asOf)return {status:'insufficient',reason:'TAIEX_DATE_NOT_ALIGNED',observations:rows.length};
+ const ma=avg(rows.slice(-240).map(r=>r.value)),previousMa=avg(rows.slice(-241,-1).map(r=>r.value)),price=rows.at(-1).value;
+ return {status:'ok',date:asOf,ma,previousMa,price,draftPoints:price>ma&&ma>previousMa?1:price<ma&&ma<previousMa?-1:0,source:entry.source};
+}
+export function previewHoldingRange({score,stepPp=null,baseRange=[60,70],floorRange=[20,30]}={}){
+ if(!finite(score)||!finite(stepPp)||stepPp<=0)return {status:'policy_pending',baseRange,floorRange,suggestedRange:null,applied:false,reason:'每分換算百分點、總分級距與回測尚未核准；不自行新增數字'};
+ return {status:'draft_simulation',baseRange,floorRange,stepPp,suggestedRange:baseRange.map((n,i)=>Math.min(n,Math.max(floorRange[i],n+score*stepPp))),applied:false};
+}
+export function buildAdvisoryDecision({stock,panels,sectorContext,macroRows=[],marketRows=[],crossSnapshot=null,existingRating=null,veto=null,gates=[],freshness={}}){
+ const asOf=panels.asOf,all=[...macroRows,...marketRows],get=id=>all.find(r=>r.id===id),axis=panels.dollarZeroAxis;
+ const rotation=attackDefense(sectorContext),credit=changes(get('BAMLH0A0HYM2'),20,asOf),annualLine=marketAnnualLine(crossSnapshot,asOf);
+ const breadth=sectorContext?.breadth?.sectorBreadth;
+ const globalFactors=[
+  factor('dxy','美元零軸',axis.value,axis.status==='ok'?(axis.value>0?-1:axis.value<-100?1:0):null,'草案：>0 −1；<−100 +1；其間0',{date:axis.date,source:axis.source,reason:axis.reason}),
+  factor('rotation','美股攻守零軸',rotation.value,rotation.status==='ok'?Math.sign(rotation.value):null,'草案：攻擊型平均−防守型平均；正+1／負−1',{date:rotation.date,source:rotation.source,detail:rotation}),
+  factor('credit','高收益債利差20日變化',credit.value,null,'「明顯擴大」幅度未定；先列變化，不替缺門檻填0',{date:credit.date,source:credit.source,detail:credit,reason:credit.reason||'THRESHOLD_PENDING'}),
+  factor('annual_line','台股240日線',annualLine.price,annualLine.status==='ok'?annualLine.draftPoints:null,'草案：站上且線向上+1；跌破且線向下−1',{detail:annualLine,date:annualLine.date,source:annualLine.source,reason:annualLine.reason}),
+  factor('us_breadth','美股11類股200日寬度',breadth?.above,breadth?.complete?(breadth.above>=9?1:breadth.above<=4?-1:0):null,'草案：≥9 +1；≤4 −1；缺類股不視為均線下',{coverage:breadth?.coverage??0,total:11}),
+  factor('sentiment','情緒合併訊號',null,null,'極度恐懼至少2項；各門檻未校準、來源未接齊，不自動加分',{reason:'THRESHOLDS_AND_SOURCE_COVERAGE_PENDING'})
+ ];
+ const t=panels.movingAverages,r=panels.revenue,e=panels.quarterEps,l=panels.lending,pb=panels.priceBook,linked=panels.linkedUSSector;
+ const inst=stock.marketActivity,dateAligned=inst?.date===asOf,net=dateAligned&&finite(inst?.institutional5dNet)?inst.institutional5dNet:null;
+ const stockFactors=[
+  factor('moving_averages','均線強弱',t.score,t.status==='ok'?t.score:null,'草案：四條均線各−1/0/+1，合計−4～+4',{date:t.date}),
+  factor('revenue_high','月營收觀測區間新高',r.newHigh,null,'新高已計算；加分權重待核，不宣稱全歷史新高',{date:r.period,reason:'WEIGHT_PENDING'}),
+  factor('eps_high','季EPS觀測區間新高',e.newHigh,null,'新高已計算；加分權重待核，與EPS surprise分開',{date:e.date,reason:'WEIGHT_PENDING'}),
+  factor('revenue_yoy','連續3月營收YoY為正',r.threePositiveYoY,null,'資料連續性已檢查；加分權重待核',{date:r.period,reason:'WEIGHT_PENDING'}),
+  factor('institutional','法人近5日合計',net,net===null?null:Math.sign(net),'草案：合計買超+1／賣超−1',{date:dateAligned?asOf:inst?.date||null,source:inst?.source}),
+  factor('lending','借券賣出連續增加',l.balanceShares,null,'3/5/10日各保留實際檢查；採哪個窗口、扣分門檻待核',{date:l.date,detail:l.deltas||null,reason:'WINDOW_AND_WEIGHT_PENDING'}),
+  factor('valuation_band','自身P/B歷史位置',pb.percentileRank,null,'循環股P/B區間已計算；五年覆蓋、適用分類、分級門檻待核',{date:pb.date,reason:'APPLICABILITY_AND_THRESHOLD_PENDING'}),
+  factor('linked_sector','對應美股板塊',linked.symbol||null,linked.status==='OK'&&finite(linked.draftContribution)?linked.draftContribution:null,'草案：三期間均領先+1／均落後−1；對應不明不給分',{date:linked.observedAt,source:linked.source})
+ ];
+ const summarize=factors=>{const known=factors.filter(r=>finite(r.draftPoints)),complete=known.length===factors.length;return {knownSubtotal:known.reduce((s,r)=>s+r.draftPoints,0),total:complete?known.reduce((s,r)=>s+r.draftPoints,0):null,known:known.length,required:factors.length,complete};};
+ const globalSummary=summarize(globalFactors),stockSummary=summarize(stockFactors),fq=stock.financialQuality||{},cashRisk=fq.cfoToNetIncome?.value<0||fq.freeCashFlow?.value<0;
+ const triggered=veto?.status==='triggered';
+ const conclusion=triggered?'既有否決已觸發，保留剔除結論。':cashRisk?'維持保守觀察，先追蹤獲利轉成現金的能力。':existingRating?.available?`沿用既有${existingRating.grade}級觀察，補充因子尚未改級。`:'資料尚未足夠，先保留觀察，暫不產出新增評級。';
+ const reasons=[];
+ if(cashRisk)reasons.push(`CFO／淨利 ${finite(fq.cfoToNetIncome?.value)?fq.cfoToNetIncome.value.toFixed(2):'缺值'}倍；FCF ${finite(fq.freeCashFlow?.value)?(fq.freeCashFlow.value/1e8).toFixed(2):'缺值'}億元；此為已核算警訊。`);
+ if(existingRating?.available)reasons.push(`既有自訂退避評級 ${existingRating.grade}；原始Gate限制與未知否決條件仍保留。`);
+ reasons.push(t.status==='ok'?`四條均線觀測強弱 ${t.score>=0?'+':''}${t.score}/4，屬草案參考。`:`均線完整性不足：${t.reason||'待補'}。`);
+ const gaps=[...globalFactors,...stockFactors].filter(f=>f.draftPoints===null).map(f=>({id:f.id,label:f.label,reason:f.reason||'DATA_OR_POLICY_PENDING'}));
+ const dataChecks=[t.status==='ok',r.status==='ok',e.status==='ok',l.status==='ok',pb.status==='ok',panels.industryMapping.status==='ok',axis.status==='ok',sectorContext?.status==='OK',freshness.stockFresh===true];
+ return {version:'advisory-decision-draft-v1',approved:false,activeScoreContribution:0,asOf,conclusion,confidence:{label:dataChecks.every(Boolean)?'所列觀測已齊，策略未校準':'受資料缺口限制',coverage:dataChecks.filter(Boolean).length,total:dataChecks.length,isReturnProbability:false},reasons:reasons.slice(0,3),changeConditions:['最新季度現金流與財報警訊依實際財報重新核算。','補齊原Gate可追溯來源；草案門檻完成回測及擁有者核准後另行整合。','行情、官方產業分類與來源時效更新後重新計算，未知不視為安全。'],discipline:'均線變化已列強弱；分批比例由投資人自行決定，未新增停損規則。',globalFactors,stockFactors,globalSummary,stockSummary,holding:previewHoldingRange({score:globalSummary.total}),gold:{informationalOnly:true,activeContribution:0,zeroAxis:panels.goldZeroAxis},sentiment:{status:'partial',vix:validRow(get('VIXCLS'),asOf)?get('VIXCLS').value:null,gaps:['VIX極高門檻待回測','VIX3M、NAAIM、Cboe股票Put/Call未有已核對輸入','FINRA月度融資轉折、全市場60日線比例、估計融資維持率須獨立來源與門檻']},gaps,originalGateWarnings:gates.filter(g=>['warning','partial','blocked','needs_user_records'].includes(g.status)).map(g=>({id:g.id,status:g.status})),appliedToRating:false,appliedToRiskBudget:false};
+}
diff --git a/worker/panel-calculations.mjs b/worker/panel-calculations.mjs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..c6156cf
--- /dev/null
+++ b/worker/panel-calculations.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,75 @@
+/** Advisory calculations only. No writes to Gate scores, weights or risk budgets. */
+const number=x=>x!==null&&x!==undefined&&x!==''&&Number.isFinite(Number(x))?Number(x):null;
+const day=s=>typeof s==='string'&&/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(s)&&Number.isFinite(Date.parse(s))&&new Date(s).toISOString().slice(0,10)===s;
+const mean=a=>a.reduce((s,x)=>s+x,0)/a.length;
+const missing=(reason,extra={})=>({status:'insufficient',reason,...extra});
+function series(rows,key='value',asOf='9999-12-31'){
+  const map=new Map();
+  for(const row of rows||[]){const value=number(row?.[key]);if(!day(row?.date)||row.date>asOf||value===null)continue;if(map.has(row.date)&&map.get(row.date).value!==value)return [];map.set(row.date,{date:row.date,value});}
+  return [...map.values()].sort((a,b)=>a.date.localeCompare(b.date));
+}
+export function movingAverageStrength(rows,{asOf=null,aligned=true}={}){
+  const data=series(rows,'close',asOf||'9999-12-31').filter(r=>r.value>0),date=data.at(-1)?.date||null;
+  const valid=aligned&&(!asOf||date===asOf),last=data.at(-1)?.value;
+  const averages=[5,10,20,60].map(sessions=>{
+    if(!valid||data.length<sessions+1)return {sessions,status:'insufficient',ma:null,previousMa:null,contribution:null};
+    const ma=mean(data.slice(-sessions).map(r=>r.value)),previousMa=mean(data.slice(-sessions-1,-1).map(r=>r.value));
+    const contribution=last>ma&&ma>previousMa?1:last<ma&&ma<previousMa?-1:0;
+    return {sessions,status:'ok',ma,previousMa,slope:ma-previousMa,contribution};
+  });
+  const complete=averages.every(r=>r.status==='ok');
+  return {status:complete?'ok':'insufficient',date,score:complete?averages.reduce((s,r)=>s+r.contribution,0):null,range:[-4,4],averages,observations:data.length,reason:complete?null:!valid?'PRICE_HISTORY_NOT_ALIGNED':'REQUIRE_61_OBSERVED_SESSIONS',scoreContribution:0};
+}
+export function zeroAxis(rows,{id,asOf=null}={}){
+  const data=series(rows,'value',asOf||'9999-12-31');
+  const at=i=>{if(i<20)return missing('REQUIRE_21_OBSERVED_SESSIONS');const previous=data.slice(i-20,i).map(r=>r.value),high=Math.max(...previous),low=Math.min(...previous);return high===low?missing('FLAT_PRIOR_RANGE',{date:data[i].date}):{status:'ok',date:data[i].date,value:(data[i].value-high)/(high-low)*100,priorHigh:high,priorLow:low};};
+  const current=at(data.length-1),fiveSessionsAgo=data.length>=26?at(data.length-6):missing('REQUIRE_26_OBSERVED_SESSIONS');
+  return {...current,id,previous5:fiveSessionsAgo,observations:data.length,referenceLines:[0,-100],tickStep:10,method:'(close - highest prior20 closes) / (highest prior20 - lowest prior20) * 100; today excluded',scoreContribution:0};
+}
+export function revenueHigh(rows,{asOf='9999-12-31'}={}){
+  const map=new Map();
+  for(const r of rows||[]){const y=Number(r.revenue_year),m=Number(r.revenue_month),value=number(r.revenue);if(!Number.isInteger(y)||y<1900||!Number.isInteger(m)||m<1||m>12||value===null||value<0)continue;const period=`${y}-${String(m).padStart(2,'0')}`;if(period>asOf.slice(0,7))continue;const old=map.get(y*12+m);if(old&&old.value!==value)return missing('CONFLICTING_MONTHLY_REVENUE');map.set(y*12+m,{period,value,publishedAt:day(r.create_time)?r.create_time:null});}
+  const keys=[...map.keys()].sort((a,b)=>a-b),key=keys.at(-1),latest=map.get(key),prior=keys.slice(0,-1).map(k=>map.get(k).value);
+  if(!latest)return missing('MONTHLY_REVENUE_NOT_CONNECTED');
+  const consecutiveThreeYoY=[key-2,key-1,key].map(k=>{const a=map.get(k),b=map.get(k-12);return {period:a?.period||null,yoy:a&&b?.value>0?(a.value/b.value-1)*100:null};});
+  const yoYComplete=consecutiveThreeYoY.every(r=>r.yoy!==null);
+  const complete=prior.length>=12;
+  return {status:complete?'ok':'insufficient',reason:complete?null:'REQUIRE_12_PRIOR_MONTHS',period:latest.period,publishedAt:latest.publishedAt,value:latest.value,newHigh:complete?latest.value>Math.max(...prior):null,priorHigh:prior.length?Math.max(...prior):null,threePositiveYoY:yoYComplete?consecutiveThreeYoY.every(r=>r.yoy>0):null,consecutiveThreeYoY,from:map.get(keys[0]).period,observations:keys.length,scope:'observed_window_only_not_all_time',source:'FinMind TaiwanStockMonthRevenue (MOPS aggregation)'};
+}
+export function quarterEpsHigh(rows,{basis=null,asOf='9999-12-31'}={}){
+  if(!['single_quarter','ytd'].includes(basis))return missing('EPS_PERIOD_BASIS_UNKNOWN');
+  const raw=series(rows,'value',asOf).filter(r=>['03-31','06-30','09-30','12-31'].includes(r.date.slice(5))),by=new Map(raw.map(r=>[r.date,r]));
+  const values=[];
+  for(const r of raw){const quarter=Math.ceil(Number(r.date.slice(5,7))/3),previousDate=`${r.date.slice(0,4)}-${['','03-31','06-30','09-30'][quarter-1]}`,previous=by.get(previousDate);if(basis==='ytd'&&quarter>1&&!previous)continue;values.push({...r,value:r.value-(basis==='ytd'&&quarter>1?previous.value:0)});}
+  const current=values.at(-1),prior=values.slice(0,-1),complete=prior.length>=4;
+  return current?{status:complete?'ok':'insufficient',reason:complete?null:'REQUIRE_4_PRIOR_QUARTERS',date:current.date,value:current.value,newHigh:complete?current.value>Math.max(...prior.map(r=>r.value)):null,priorHigh:prior.length?Math.max(...prior.map(r=>r.value)):null,from:values[0].date,observations:values.length,basis:'single_quarter',inputBasis:basis,scope:'observed_window_only_not_all_time',source:'FinMind quarterly EPS; corporate actions adjusted'}:missing('QUARTERLY_EPS_NOT_CONNECTED');
+}
+export function priceBookBand(rows,{asOf,requestedFrom=null}={}){
+  const data=series(rows.map(r=>({date:r.date,value:r.PBR??r.pb??r.value})),'value',asOf).filter(r=>r.value>0),current=data.findLast(r=>r.date===asOf);
+  if(!current||data.length<250)return missing(!current?'SAME_DAY_PBR_MISSING':'REQUIRE_250_PBR_OBSERVATIONS',{observations:data.length,date:asOf||null});
+  const values=data.map(r=>r.value).sort((a,b)=>a-b),percentile=p=>{const i=(values.length-1)*p,a=Math.floor(i),b=Math.ceil(i);return values[a]+(values[b]-values[a])*(i-a);};
+  return {status:'ok',date:asOf,current:current.value,p20:percentile(.2),median:percentile(.5),p80:percentile(.8),percentileRank:values.filter(x=>x<=current.value).length/values.length*100,from:data[0].date,through:data.at(-1).date,requestedFrom,observations:data.length,source:'FinMind TaiwanStockPER PBR; TWSE/TPEx aggregation',scope:'available_observed_window_not_guaranteed_five_years',scoreContribution:0};
+}
+export const INDUSTRY_SECTORS=Object.freeze({'01':'XLB','02':'XLP','03':'XLB','04':'XLY','05':'XLI','06':'XLI','08':'XLB','09':'XLB','10':'XLB','11':'XLY','12':'XLY','15':'XLI','16':'XLY','17':'XLF','18':'XLY','21':'XLB','22':'XLV','24':'XLK','25':'XLK','26':'XLK','28':'XLK','29':'XLK','30':'XLK','31':'XLK','32':'XLC','33':'XLP','35':'XLI','36':'XLK','37':'XLY','38':'XLY'});
+export function mapIndustry(issuer,{asOf='9999-12-31'}={}){
+  const code=String(issuer?.industryCode||'').padStart(2,'0');
+  const trusted=issuer?.status==='official'&&day(issuer.date)&&issuer.date<=asOf&&/^(TWSE|TPEx)$/.test(issuer.market)&&['openapi.twse.com.tw','mopsfin.twse.com.tw','www.tpex.org.tw'].some(host=>{try{return new URL(issuer.sourceUrl).hostname===host;}catch{return false;}});
+  if(!trusted)return missing('OFFICIAL_INDUSTRY_MISSING_OR_FUTURE',{sector:null});
+  const business=issuer.businessEvidence,hasBusiness=business?.status==='verified'&&day(business.date)&&business.date<=asOf&&typeof business.sourceUrl==='string'&&/^https:\/\/(?:doc\.twse\.com\.tw|mopsov\.twse\.com\.tw|mops\.twse\.com\.tw)\//.test(business.sourceUrl);
+  const text=hasBusiness?String(business.text||''):'';
+  let sector=/無塵室.*工程|廠務工程|工程承攬/.test(text)?'XLI':INDUSTRY_SECTORS[code]||null;
+  if(code==='14')sector=/工程/.test(text)?'XLI':/不動產.*(?:開發|租賃)|住宅.*開發/.test(text)?'XLRE':null;
+  if(code==='27')sector=/電信服務|行動通訊服務/.test(text)?'XLC':/網通設備|通訊設備/.test(text)?'XLK':null;
+  if(code==='23')sector=/發電|配電|供水|公用事業/.test(text)?'XLU':/石油|天然氣.*開採|燃料/.test(text)?'XLE':null;
+  if(code==='31'&&!hasBusiness)sector=null; // Other-electronics includes engineering; do not silently map a known mixed category to technology.
+  return {status:sector?'ok':'ambiguous',sector,industryCode:code,industry:issuer.industryName||code,date:issuer.date,sourceUrl:issuer.sourceUrl,businessEvidence:hasBusiness?business:null,reason:sector?null:'MIXED_OR_AMBIGUOUS_OFFICIAL_INDUSTRY',draft:true,scoreContribution:0};
+}
+export function buildStockPanels(stock,marketRows=[],sectorContext=null,{asOf=stock.price?.date}={}){
+  asOf=day(asOf)?asOf:'0000-01-01';
+  const inputs=stock.panelInputs||{},ma=movingAverageStrength(stock.priceHistory||[],{asOf,aligned:stock.technical?.historyState!=='UNKNOWN'}),mapping=mapIndustry(inputs.issuer,{asOf});
+  const dxy=marketRows.find(r=>r.id==='ICE_DXY'),gold=marketRows.find(r=>r.id==='XAU');
+  const available=r=>r&&['ok','snapshot'].includes(r.status)&&!r.is_stale&&day(r.date)&&r.date<=asOf;
+  const axis=r=>available(r)&&r.history?.at(-1)?.date===r.date?{...zeroAxis(r.history,{id:r.id,asOf}),source:r.source,sourceDate:r.date}:missing('SOURCE_MISSING_STALE_OR_NOT_ALIGNED',{id:r?.id||null});
+  const sector=mapping.sector?sectorContext?.sectors?.[mapping.sector]:null;
+  return {version:'panel-calculations-v1',symbol:stock.id,asOf,draft:true,pointInTimeVerified:false,activeScoreContribution:0,movingAverages:ma,revenue:revenueHigh(inputs.revenueHistory||stock.valuationInputs?.revenueHistory||[],{asOf}),quarterEps:quarterEpsHigh(stock.epsHistory?.quarterly||[],{basis:inputs.epsBasis,asOf}),lending:inputs.lending||missing('LENDING_SOLD_BALANCE_NOT_CONNECTED'),priceBook:priceBookBand(inputs.pbrHistory||[],{asOf,requestedFrom:inputs.pbrRequestedFrom}),industryMapping:mapping,linkedUSSector:sector?{...sector,symbol:mapping.sector,source:sectorContext.source,observedAt:sectorContext.observedAt,draftContribution:sector.classification==='LEADING'?1:sector.classification==='LAGGING'?-1:sector.status==='OK'?0:null}:missing('MAPPED_SECTOR_SOURCE_UNAVAILABLE'),dollarZeroAxis:axis(dxy),goldZeroAxis:{...axis(gold),informationalOnly:true,activeScoreContribution:0}};
+}
diff --git a/worker/panel-display.js b/worker/panel-display.js
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..6ceb858
--- /dev/null
+++ b/worker/panel-display.js
@@ -0,0 +1,48 @@
+/* ADVISORY_PANEL_DISPLAY_START */
+function advisoryAxis(host,row,label,{min=-100,max=20,step=10}={}){
+ const value=row?.status==='ok'&&Number.isFinite(row.value)?row.value:null;
+ if(value===null)return;
+ const lower=Math.min(min,Math.floor(value/step)*step),upper=Math.max(max,Math.ceil(value/step)*step),span=upper-lower;
+ const displayStep=Math.max(step,Math.ceil(span/80/step)*step);
+ const chart=document.createElement('div');chart.className='advisory-axis';chart.setAttribute('role','img');chart.setAttribute('aria-label',`${label} ${value.toFixed(2)}；零軸0；顯示範圍${lower}至${upper}；每格${displayStep}`);
+ const ticks=new Set([lower,upper,0,...(lower<=-100?[-100]:[])]);for(let tick=lower;tick<=upper;tick+=displayStep)ticks.add(tick);
+ for(const tick of [...ticks].sort((a,b)=>a-b)){const line=document.createElement('span');line.className='advisory-axis-tick';line.style.left=`${(tick-lower)/span*100}%`;if(tick===0||tick===-100||tick===lower||tick===upper){line.className+=' reference';line.textContent=String(tick);}chart.append(line);}
+ const bar=document.createElement('b'),zero=(0-lower)/span*100,position=(value-lower)/span*100;bar.className='advisory-axis-bar';bar.style.left=`${Math.min(zero,position)}%`;bar.style.width=`${Math.abs(position-zero)}%`;bar.style.background=value<0?'var(--blue)':'var(--yellow)';chart.append(bar);host.append(chart);
+}
+function renderAdvisoryPanels(model){
+ const host=document.getElementById('advisoryPanels');if(!host)return;
+ host.replaceChildren();const p=model?.report?.advisoryPanels;
+ if(!p||model.symbol&&p.symbol&&p.symbol!==model.symbol){host.hidden=true;return;}host.hidden=false;
+ const title=document.createElement('h4');title.textContent='補充觀測｜計算已接入，評分門檻為草案';host.append(title);
+ const note=document.createElement('p');note.className='tiny';note.textContent=`${model.symbol||''}｜台股基準 ${p.asOf||'缺值'}。新增觀測不改既有總分、Gate 或風險預算；缺資料保留缺項。`;host.append(note);
+ if(model.report?.advisoryDecision)renderAdvisoryDecision(host,model.report.advisoryDecision);
+ const observations=document.createElement('details'),openLabel=document.createElement('summary');openLabel.textContent='展開補充觀測與計算';observations.append(openLabel);host.append(observations);
+ const grid=document.createElement('div');grid.className='advisory-grid';observations.append(grid);
+ const show=x=>Number.isFinite(x)?x.toFixed(2):'缺值';
+ function card(label,body,source){const item=document.createElement('section'),h=document.createElement('b'),text=document.createElement('p');item.className='advisory-card';h.textContent=label;text.textContent=body;item.append(h,text);if(source){const n=document.createElement('small');n.textContent=source;item.append(n);}grid.append(item);return item;}
+ const ma=p.movingAverages||{};
+ const maCard=card('均線強弱 −4～+4',`${ma.score===null||ma.score===undefined?'樣本不足':(ma.score>=0?'+':'')+ma.score}｜${(ma.averages||[]).map(x=>`${x.sessions}日 MA ${show(x.ma)}／${x.contribution===null?'缺值':x.contribution>0?'+1':String(x.contribution)}`).join('；')}`,`觀測 ${ma.date||'缺值'}；${ma.observations||0}筆；須同時滿足價位與均線斜率。`);
+ if(Number.isFinite(ma.score))advisoryAxis(maCard,{status:'ok',value:ma.score},'均線強弱',{min:-4,max:4,step:1});
+ const rev=p.revenue||{};card('月營收創高',`${rev.newHigh===true?'觀測區間創高':rev.newHigh===false?'未創觀測區間新高':'資料不足'}｜${rev.period||'月份缺值'}；近3月YoY連續為正：${rev.threePositiveYoY===null||rev.threePositiveYoY===undefined?'待確認':rev.threePositiveYoY?'是':'否'}`,`${rev.from||'起月缺值'}起 ${rev.observations||0}月；${rev.source||rev.reason||''}；不是全歷史紀錄。`);
+ const eps=p.quarterEps||{};card('季 EPS 創高',`${eps.newHigh===true?'觀測區間創高':eps.newHigh===false?'未創觀測區間新高':'資料不足'}｜${eps.date||'期別缺值'} EPS ${show(eps.value)}`,`${eps.observations||0}季；${eps.source||eps.reason||''}；單季、已調整股數口徑，不代表季度共識。`);
+ const lend=p.lending||{};card('借券賣出餘額',`${lend.status==='ok'?Number(lend.balanceShares).toLocaleString('zh-TW')+' 股':'待確認'}｜占同日發行股數 ${show(lend.balanceRatioPct)}%`,`${lend.date||'日期缺值'}；${lend.market||''}；${lend.history?.length||0}筆已保存日資料；${lend.sourceUrl||lend.reason||''}${lend.ratioReason?'；'+lend.ratioReason:''}`);
+ const pb=p.priceBook||{};card('股價淨值比歷史區間',pb.status==='ok'?`P/B ${show(pb.current)} 倍｜P20 ${show(pb.p20)}／中位 ${show(pb.median)}／P80 ${show(pb.p80)}`:`待補：${pb.reason||'缺值'}`,`${pb.from||'起日缺值'}～${pb.through||p.asOf||'迄日缺值'}；${pb.observations||0}筆；自身可得樣本，不代替既有P/E。`);
+ const dxy=p.dollarZeroAxis||{};const dxCard=card('美元指數零軸',`目前 ${show(dxy.value)}｜5交易日前 ${show(dxy.previous5?.value)}；0＝前20日最高，−100＝前20日最低`,`${dxy.date||dxy.sourceDate||'日期缺值'}；${dxy.source||dxy.reason||'缺值'}；前20日不含今日。`);advisoryAxis(dxCard,dxy,'美元指數零軸');
+ const mapping=p.industryMapping||{},sector=p.linkedUSSector||{};card('自動對應美股板塊',mapping.sector?`${mapping.industry||mapping.industryCode} → ${mapping.sector}｜${sector.classification||'強弱缺值'}`:`對應不明：${mapping.reason||'缺官方產業別'}`,`${mapping.date||'產業日期缺值'}；${mapping.sourceUrl||''}；新增±1僅試算，未納入正式評級。`);
+ const breadth=model.report?.usSectorContext?.breadth;
+ if(breadth){const s=breadth.sectorBreadth,g=breadth.globalBreadth;card('美股與全球寬度',`美股類股 ${s.above}/${s.coverage} 在200日線上（涵蓋${s.coverage}/11）；全球 ${g.spyAvailable?`${g.above}/${g.coverage}`:'SPY缺值，不算比例'}（涵蓋${g.coverage}/10）；RSP/SPY20日 ${show(breadth.equalWeightRatio20d)}%`,`Tiingo adjClose；每市場一票；全球缺${g.missing.join('、')||'無'}；非S&P500成分股廣度。`);}
+ const gold=p.goldZeroAxis||{};card('黃金背景參考',`金價零軸 ${show(gold.value)}｜5交易日前 ${show(gold.previous5?.value)}；不計分`,`${gold.date||'日期缺值'}；${gold.source||gold.reason||'缺值'}。`);
+}
+function renderAdvisoryDecision(host,decision){
+ const box=document.createElement('section');box.className='advisory-decision';
+ const title=document.createElement('b');title.textContent='綜合觀察｜草案未啟用';const text=document.createElement('p');text.textContent=decision.conclusion;box.append(title,text);
+ const confidence=document.createElement('p');confidence.className='tiny';confidence.textContent=`核對信心：${decision.confidence.label}（所列觀測${decision.confidence.coverage}/${decision.confidence.total}，不是報酬勝率）。`;box.append(confidence);
+ const reasons=document.createElement('ul');for(const reason of decision.reasons||[]){const line=document.createElement('li');line.textContent=reason;reasons.append(line);}box.append(reasons);
+ const holding=document.createElement('p'),h=decision.holding;holding.textContent=`建議持股區間：${h.suggestedRange?h.suggestedRange.join('～')+'%（僅模擬）':'待門檻核准，尚未產出'}；基準${h.baseRange.join('～')}%，下限${h.floorRange.join('～')}%。${h.reason||''}`;box.append(holding);
+ const totals=document.createElement('p');totals.className='tiny';totals.textContent=`草案總經因子已知部分合計 ${decision.globalSummary.knownSubtotal}（${decision.globalSummary.known}/${decision.globalSummary.required}）；個股因子已知部分 ${decision.stockSummary.knownSubtotal}（${decision.stockSummary.known}/${decision.stockSummary.required}）。未決項目不當0分，未改正式總分或風險預算。`;box.append(totals);
+ const details=document.createElement('details'),label=document.createElement('summary');label.textContent='查看加減分草案、來源與缺口';details.append(label);
+ for(const f of [...decision.globalFactors,...decision.stockFactors]){const row=document.createElement('p');row.className='tiny';row.textContent=`${f.label}｜觀測 ${f.value===null||f.value===undefined?'缺值':String(f.value)}｜草案 ${f.draftPoints===null?'未定／缺資料':f.draftPoints>0?'+'+f.draftPoints:String(f.draftPoints)}｜${f.date||'日期缺值'}｜${f.rule}${f.reason?'；'+f.reason:''}`;if(f.source){try{const u=new URL(f.source);if(u.protocol==='https:'){const link=document.createElement('a');link.href=u.href;link.target='_blank';link.rel='noopener noreferrer';link.textContent=' 來源';row.append(link);}}catch{}}details.append(row);}
+ for(const change of decision.changeConditions||[]){const row=document.createElement('p');row.className='tiny';row.textContent='何時改變觀察：'+change;details.append(row);}box.append(details);
+ const discipline=document.createElement('p');discipline.className='tiny';discipline.textContent=decision.discipline;box.append(discipline);host.append(box);
+}
+/* ADVISORY_PANEL_DISPLAY_END */
diff --git a/worker/panel-site.js b/worker/panel-site.js
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..a93e647
--- /dev/null
+++ b/worker/panel-site.js
@@ -0,0 +1,13 @@
+async function attachPanelSources(stock,env={}){
+  if(!stock?.price?.date||!env.DB)return stock;
+  const inputs={...stock.panelInputs},registry=await getOfficialIssuedShares(env),current=registry.data.get(stock.id);
+  // Current classification never reconstructs a past information set.
+  inputs.issuer=current?.date&&current.date<=stock.price.date?current:inputs.issuer||null;
+  try{
+    const read=key=>readRuntimeCache(env,key),write=(key,value,ttl)=>writeRuntimeCache(env,key,value,ttl);
+    const pool=await PANEL_SOURCES.collectLending({asOf:stock.price.date,read,write});
+    const officialMarket=inputs.issuer?.market||stock.quoteIntegration?.price?.market;
+    inputs.lending=await PANEL_SOURCES.selectAndSaveLending({pool,symbol:stock.id,market:officialMarket,asOf:stock.price.date,issuer:inputs.issuer,priceDates:(stock.priceHistory||[]).map(r=>r.date),read,write});
+  }catch{inputs.lending={status:'insufficient',reason:'OFFICIAL_LENDING_COLLECTION_FAILED',history:[]};}
+  return {...stock,panelInputs:inputs};
+}
diff --git a/worker/panel-sources.mjs b/worker/panel-sources.mjs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..978f838
--- /dev/null
+++ b/worker/panel-sources.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,49 @@
+/** Official lending-sold data. No substituted margin-short or total-lending balances. */
+export const TPEX_LENDING_URL='https://www.tpex.org.tw/openapi/v1/tpex_margin_sbl';
+export const TWSE_LENDING_BASE='https://www.twse.com.tw/rwd/zh/marginTrading/TWT93U';
+const day=s=>typeof s==='string'&&/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(s)&&Number.isFinite(Date.parse(s))&&new Date(s).toISOString().slice(0,10)===s;
+export function officialDay(raw){const s=String(raw||'').trim();const date=/^\d{7}$/.test(s)?`${Number(s.slice(0,3))+1911}-${s.slice(3,5)}-${s.slice(5)}`:/^\d{8}$/.test(s)?`${s.slice(0,4)}-${s.slice(4,6)}-${s.slice(6)}`:s;return day(date)?date:null;}
+const quantity=s=>typeof s==='number'?Number.isSafeInteger(s)&&s>=0?s:null:/^\d[\d,]*$/.test(String(s||'').trim())?Number(String(s).replaceAll(',','')):null;
+export function parseLending(payload,market,{capturedAt,sourceUrl,asOf}={}){
+  const records={};
+  if(market==='TWSE'){
+    if(payload?.stat!=='OK'||officialDay(payload.date)!==asOf||payload.hints!=='單位：股'||payload.fields?.length!==15||payload.fields[8]!=='前日餘額'||payload.fields[12]!=='當日餘額'||payload.groups?.[2]?.title!=='借券賣出'||payload.groups[1]?.span!==6||payload.groups[2]?.span!==6)throw Error('TWSE_LENDING_SCHEMA_OR_DATE');
+    for(const r of payload.data||[]){const symbol=String(r[0]),balanceShares=quantity(r[12]),previousShares=quantity(r[8]);if(/^\d{4,6}$/.test(symbol)&&balanceShares!==null)records[symbol]={symbol,market,date:asOf,balanceShares,previousShares,soldShares:quantity(r[9]),returnedShares:quantity(r[10]),adjustedShares:quantity(r[11]),unit:'shares',capturedAt,sourceUrl};}
+  }else if(market==='TPEx'){
+    if(!Array.isArray(payload))throw Error('TPEX_LENDING_SCHEMA');
+    for(const r of payload){const symbol=String(r.SecuritiesCompanyCode||''),date=officialDay(r.Date),balanceShares=quantity(r.SecuritiesBorrowingBalanceOfTheMarketDay);if(/^\d{4,6}$/.test(symbol)&&date&&date<=asOf&&balanceShares!==null)records[symbol]={symbol,market,date,balanceShares,previousShares:quantity(r.SecuritiesBorrowingBalancePreviousDay),soldShares:quantity(r.SecuritiesBorrowingSale),returnedShares:quantity(r.SecuritiesBorrowingReturn),adjustedShares:quantity(r.SecuritiesBorrowingAdjustment),unit:'shares',capturedAt,sourceUrl};}
+  }else throw Error('LENDING_MARKET_UNKNOWN');
+  if(!Object.keys(records).length)throw Error('LENDING_NO_ELIGIBLE_ROWS');
+  return records;
+}
+const tasks=new Map();
+export async function collectLending({asOf,read,write,fetchImpl=fetch,now=Date.now()}){
+  if(!day(asOf)||asOf>new Date(now+8*3600000).toISOString().slice(0,10))throw Error('LENDING_DATE_INVALID');
+  const key=`panel:lending-pool:v1:${asOf}`,old=await read(key);
+  if(old?.fresh)return old.payload;
+  if(tasks.has(asOf))return tasks.get(asOf);
+  const task=(async()=>{
+    const stocks={},errors=[];
+    const urls=[['TWSE',`${TWSE_LENDING_BASE}?date=${asOf.replaceAll('-','')}&response=json`],['TPEx',TPEX_LENDING_URL]];
+    const results=await Promise.all(urls.map(async([market,sourceUrl])=>{
+      try{const r=await fetchImpl(sourceUrl,{headers:{Accept:'application/json'},signal:AbortSignal.timeout(4500)});if(!r.ok)throw Error(`HTTP_${r.status}`);return {market,records:parseLending(await r.json(),market,{asOf,sourceUrl,capturedAt:new Date(now).toISOString()})};}
+      catch(error){return {market,error:/^HTTP_\d+$/.test(error.message)?error.message:'OFFICIAL_LENDING_UNAVAILABLE_OR_SCHEMA'};}
+    }));
+    for(const r of results)if(r.records){Object.assign(stocks,r.records);if(Object.values(r.records).every(point=>point.date<asOf))errors.push({market:r.market,code:'LATEST_SESSION_MISSING'});}else errors.push({market:r.market,code:r.error});
+    const payload={asOf,checkedAt:new Date(now).toISOString(),stocks,errors,status:errors.length?'partial':'ok'};
+    if(!await write(key,payload,errors.length?3600000:6*3600000))return {...payload,storageStatus:'failed'};
+    return {...payload,storageStatus:'saved'};
+  })().finally(()=>tasks.delete(asOf));tasks.set(asOf,task);return task;
+}
+export async function selectAndSaveLending({pool,symbol,market,asOf,issuer,priceDates=[],read,write}){
+  const point=pool?.stocks?.[symbol],old=(await read(`panel:lending-history:v1:${symbol}`))?.payload,history=old?.history||[];
+  if(!point||point.market!==market||point.date!==asOf)return {status:'insufficient',reason:point?'LENDING_DATE_OR_MARKET_NOT_ALIGNED':'LENDING_SOLD_BALANCE_NOT_AVAILABLE',date:point?.date||null,previous:history.at(-1)||null,history,sourceErrors:pool?.errors||[]};
+  const conflict=history.find(r=>r.date===point.date&&r.balanceShares!==point.balanceShares);
+  if(conflict)return {status:'conflict',reason:'SAME_DAY_LENDING_VALUE_CHANGED',date:point.date,history};
+  const retained=[...new Map([...history,point].map(r=>[r.date,r])).values()].sort((a,b)=>a.date.localeCompare(b.date)).slice(-90);
+  const saved=await write(`panel:lending-history:v1:${symbol}`,{symbol,history:retained},370*86400000);
+  const sameShares=issuer?.date===point.date&&Number.isFinite(issuer.shares)&&issuer.shares>0;
+  const deltas={};
+  for(const n of [1,3,5,10]){const slice=retained.slice(-n-1),dates=priceDates.filter(d=>d<=asOf).slice(-n-1);deltas[n]=slice.length===n+1&&dates.length===n+1&&slice.every((r,i)=>r.date===dates[i])?{changeShares:point.balanceShares-slice[0].balanceShares,consecutiveIncrease:slice.slice(1).every((r,i)=>r.balanceShares>slice[i].balanceShares)}:null;}
+  return {...point,status:saved?'ok':'storage_failed',balanceRatioPct:sameShares?point.balanceShares/issuer.shares*100:null,ratioReason:sameShares?null:'SAME_DAY_ISSUED_SHARES_MISSING',shareSource:sameShares?issuer.sourceUrl:null,history:retained,deltas,storageStatus:saved?'saved':'failed',scoreContribution:0};
+}
diff --git a/worker/tiingo-market.mjs b/worker/tiingo-market.mjs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..f20ab34
--- /dev/null
+++ b/worker/tiingo-market.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,81 @@
+/** Private-owner EOD adapter. Never send raw histories to a public relay or logs. */
+export const SECTORS=Object.freeze(['XLB','XLC','XLE','XLF','XLI','XLK','XLP','XLRE','XLU','XLV','XLY']);
+export const GLOBAL=Object.freeze(['SPY','EWJ','MCHI','EWU','EWC','INDA','EWT','EWG','EWQ','EWY']);
+export const SYMBOLS=Object.freeze([...new Set([...SECTORS,'SPY','RSP',...GLOBAL])]);
+export const PRICE_FIELD='adjClose';
+export const PROVIDER='tiingo-eod-private-v1';
+const DAY=86400000;
+const safeDate=s=>typeof s==='string'&&/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(s)&&Number.isFinite(Date.parse(s))&&new Date(s).toISOString().slice(0,10)===s;
+const goodHistory=h=>Array.isArray(h)&&h.length>0&&h.every((r,i)=>safeDate(r?.date)&&Number.isFinite(r.price)&&r.price>0&&(!i||r.date>h[i-1].date));
+export async function fetchTiingoDaily(symbol,{token,expectedSession,fetchImpl=fetch,now=new Date()}={}) {
+  if(!token)return {status:'source_failed',code:'not_configured'};
+  if(!SYMBOLS.includes(symbol))return {status:'source_failed',code:'invalid_symbol'};
+  if(!safeDate(expectedSession))return {status:'source_failed',code:'invalid_expected_session'};
+  const start=new Date(new Date(now).getTime()-430*DAY).toISOString().slice(0,10);
+  const url=`https://api.tiingo.com/tiingo/daily/${symbol}/prices?startDate=${start}&endDate=${expectedSession}&format=json`;
+  let response;
+  try{response=await fetchImpl(url,{headers:{Authorization:`Token ${token}`,Accept:'application/json'},signal:AbortSignal.timeout(12000)});}catch{return {status:'source_failed',code:'network_error'};}
+  if([401,403,429].includes(response.status))return {status:'source_failed',code:response.status===429?'rate_limited':'unauthorized'};
+  if(!response.ok)return {status:'source_failed',code:'upstream_http_error'};
+  let json;try{json=await response.json();}catch{return {status:'source_failed',code:'invalid_json'};}
+  if(!Array.isArray(json)||!json.length)return {status:'source_failed',code:'empty_data'};
+  const unique=new Map();let excludedFutureRows=0;
+  for(const row of json){
+    const date=typeof row?.date==='string'?row.date.slice(0,10):'';
+    // Filter BEFORE determining observedAt: an intraday/next-session bar cannot invalidate an available completed session.
+    if(safeDate(date)&&date>expectedSession){excludedFutureRows++;continue;}
+    if(!safeDate(date)||date<start||!Number.isFinite(row?.adjClose)||row.adjClose<=0)continue;
+    if(unique.has(date)&&unique.get(date).price!==row.adjClose)return {status:'source_failed',code:'conflicting_duplicate'};
+    unique.set(date,{date,price:row.adjClose});
+  }
+  const history=[...unique.values()].sort((a,b)=>a.date.localeCompare(b.date));
+  return history.length?{status:'ok',symbol,history,observedAt:history.at(-1).date,priceField:PRICE_FIELD,excludedFutureRows}:{status:'source_failed',code:'invalid_prices'};
+}
+export function selectSession(result,expectedSession,previous=null){
+  const old=previous?.status==='ok'?previous:previous?.previous||null;
+  const failed=code=>({status:'source_failed',reasonCode:code,source:'tiingo',previous:old});
+  if(!safeDate(expectedSession))return failed('invalid_expected_session');
+  if(result?.status!=='ok')return failed(result?.code||'invalid_response');
+  // Defensive second check for stored/imported adapter results as well as HTTP results.
+  const history=result.history?.filter(r=>r.date<=expectedSession);
+  if(!goodHistory(history))return failed('invalid_prices');
+  const observedAt=history.at(-1).date;
+  if(observedAt!==expectedSession)return {...failed('expected_session_missing'),receivedSession:observedAt};
+  return {...result,history,observedAt,status:'ok',source:'tiingo',priceField:PRICE_FIELD};
+}
+export function sma200(history){return goodHistory(history)&&history.length>=200?history.slice(-200).reduce((s,r)=>s+r.price,0)/200:null;}
+export function breadth(snapshots){
+  const get=s=>snapshots[s]?.status==='ok'&&goodHistory(snapshots[s].history)?snapshots[s].history:null;
+  const count=symbols=>{let above=0;const missing=[];for(const s of symbols){const h=get(s),ma=sma200(h);if(ma===null)missing.push(s);else if(h.at(-1).price>ma)above++;}return {above,coverage:symbols.length-missing.length,total:symbols.length,missing};};
+  const sectors=count(SECTORS),global=count(GLOBAL),spy=get('SPY'),rsp=get('RSP');
+  let equalWeightRatio20d=null,ratioObservedAt=null;
+  if(spy&&rsp){
+    // Require identical last 21 sessions; an intersected gap would silently change the time window.
+    const a=rsp.slice(-21),b=spy.slice(-21);
+    if(a.length===21&&b.length===21&&a.every((r,i)=>r.date===b[i].date)){
+      equalWeightRatio20d=((a.at(-1).price/b.at(-1).price)/(a[0].price/b[0].price)-1)*100;ratioObservedAt=b.at(-1).date;
+    }
+  }
+  const spyAvailable=sma200(spy)!==null;
+  return {priceField:PRICE_FIELD,sectorBreadth:{...sectors,complete:sectors.coverage===11},globalBreadth:{...global,spyAvailable,ratio:spyAvailable&&global.coverage?global.above/global.coverage:null,denominator:spyAvailable?global.coverage:null,complete:global.coverage===10},equalWeightRatio20d,ratioObservedAt,ratioDirection:equalWeightRatio20d===null?'missing':equalWeightRatio20d>0?'rising':equalWeightRatio20d<0?'falling':'flat'};
+}
+export async function refreshPrivateMarket({token,expectedSession,cache,quota,now=new Date(),fetchImpl=fetch}){
+  if(!cache?.get||!cache?.set||!quota?.reserve)throw Error('PRIVATE_PERSISTENT_CACHE_AND_ATOMIC_QUOTA_REQUIRED');
+  const snapshots={};let requests=0,stopCode=null;
+  for(const symbol of SYMBOLS){
+    const old=await cache.get(`tiingo:${symbol}`);
+    if(old?.status==='ok'&&old.observedAt===expectedSession&&old.priceField===PRICE_FIELD){snapshots[symbol]=selectSession(old,expectedSession);continue;}
+    if(old?.expectedSession===expectedSession&&Date.parse(old.retryAt)>now.getTime()){snapshots[symbol]=old;continue;}
+    let result;
+    if(!token)result={status:'source_failed',code:'not_configured'};
+    else if(stopCode)result={status:'source_failed',code:stopCode};
+    else if(!await quota.reserve(symbol,now.getTime()))result={status:'source_failed',code:'quota_guard'};
+    else{requests++;result=await fetchTiingoDaily(symbol,{token,expectedSession,now,fetchImpl});if(['unauthorized','rate_limited'].includes(result.code))stopCode=result.code;}
+    const selected=selectSession(result,expectedSession,old);
+    const resolved={...selected,expectedSession,capturedAt:now.toISOString(),...(selected.status!=='ok'?{retryAt:new Date(now.getTime()+(stopCode==='unauthorized'?12:1)*3600000).toISOString()}: {})};
+    // Store failure/backoff separately from the original last-good observation. Never move the old date.
+    if(!await cache.set(`tiingo:${symbol}`,resolved))throw Error('PRIVATE_CACHE_WRITE_FAILED');
+    snapshots[symbol]=resolved;
+  }
+  return {provider:PROVIDER,expectedSession,checkedAt:now.toISOString(),snapshots,summary:breadth(snapshots),requestCount:requests};
+}
diff --git a/worker/tiingo-site.js b/worker/tiingo-site.js
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..be22cfa
--- /dev/null
+++ b/worker/tiingo-site.js
@@ -0,0 +1,72 @@
+// Private Site adapter. This is deliberately absent from official-data-tools and public relay collectors.
+const TIINGO_PRIVATE_KEY='tiingo:private-market:v1';
+const TIINGO_SOURCE='Tiingo EOD (private owner; adjClose)';
+// Source validation guard only, NOT an investment threshold. Conservative default pending owner review.
+const TIINGO_COMPARISON_MAX_PP=.25;
+let tiingoSiteTask=null;
+function normalizeTiingoSnapshot(snapshot,now=Date.now()){
+  if(snapshot?.provider!==TIINGO_MARKET.PROVIDER)throw Error('TIINGO_PROVIDER_MISMATCH');
+  const expected=US_SECTOR_SESSION.expectedLatestSession(now),captured=Date.parse(snapshot.checkedAt||'');
+  if(!Number.isFinite(captured)||captured>now+60000)throw Error('TIINGO_CAPTURE_INVALID');
+  if(snapshot.expectedSession!==expected)throw Error('TIINGO_LATEST_SESSION_MISSING');
+  const histories={},status={};
+  for(const id of TIINGO_MARKET.SYMBOLS){
+    const entry=snapshot.snapshots?.[id],selected=TIINGO_MARKET.selectSession(entry,expected);
+    const ok=selected.status==='ok'&&entry.symbol===id&&entry.priceField==='adjClose'&&selected.history.every(r=>!US_SECTOR_SESSION.calendarCovers(r.date)||US_SECTOR_SESSION.isSession(r.date));
+    histories[id]=ok?selected.history.map(r=>({date:r.date,close:r.price})):[];
+    status[id]={status:ok?'ok':'unknown',observed_at:ok?expected:null,count:histories[id].length,reason:ok?null:entry?.reasonCode||'TIINGO_INVALID_HISTORY'};
+  }
+  if(status.SPY.status!=='ok')throw Error('TIINGO_BENCHMARK_UNAVAILABLE');
+  return {provider:TIINGO_MARKET.PROVIDER,schema_version:1,source:TIINGO_SOURCE,priceField:'adjClose',histories,status,generated_at:snapshot.checkedAt,sourceObservedAt:expected,sessionFreshness:{ok:true,observed:expected,expected},fetchError:snapshot.fetchError||null};
+}
+function tiingoBreadthForAnalysis(data,referenceDate){
+  const snapshots={};
+  for(const id of TIINGO_MARKET.SYMBOLS){const history=(data.histories[id]||[]).filter(r=>!referenceDate||r.date<referenceDate).map(r=>({date:r.date,price:r.close}));snapshots[id]={status:data.status[id]?.status==='ok'&&history.length?'ok':'source_failed',history};}
+  return TIINGO_MARKET.breadth(snapshots);
+}
+function compareMarketProviders(primary,fallback){
+  const rows=[];
+  if(primary?.sourceObservedAt!==fallback?.sourceObservedAt)return {status:'not_compared',reason:'NO_COMMON_LATEST_SESSION',rows};
+  const a=US_SECTOR_CORE.buildSectorReport(primary),b=US_SECTOR_CORE.buildSectorReport(fallback);
+  if(a.status!=='OK'||b.status!=='OK')return {status:'not_compared',reason:'INCOMPLETE_COMMON_HISTORY',rows};
+  for(const id of US_SECTOR_CORE.SECTORS)for(const n of [5,10,20]){
+    const field=`return${n}d`,differencePp=a.sectors[id].relative[field]-b.sectors[id].relative[field];
+    rows.push({symbol:id,sessions:n,differencePp});
+  }
+  const maxDifferencePp=Math.max(...rows.map(r=>Math.abs(r.differencePp)));
+  return {status:maxDifferencePp>TIINGO_COMPARISON_MAX_PP?'blocked':'within_guard',maxDifferencePp,limitPp:TIINGO_COMPARISON_MAX_PP,limitPurpose:'source_quality_guard_pending_owner_review',priceFields:{primary:'adjClose',fallback:'close'},caveat:'Yahoo close 與 Tiingo adjClose 除息附近可能不同；超過來源保護界線先停用 Tiingo 報告待核，不拼接。',rows};
+}
+async function refreshTiingoSite(env,now=Date.now()){
+  const previous=await readRuntimeCache(env,TIINGO_PRIVATE_KEY);
+  if(!env.TIINGO_API_TOKEN||!env.DB)return {...previous?.payload,fetchError:!env.TIINGO_API_TOKEN?'TIINGO_NOT_CONFIGURED':'PRIVATE_STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE'};
+  let store,owner;
+  try{
+    const expectedSession=US_SECTOR_SESSION.expectedLatestSession(now);
+    store=await TIINGO_STORE.createPrivateStore({db:env.DB,read:key=>readRuntimeCache(env,key),write:(key,value,ttl)=>writeRuntimeCache(env,key,value,ttl)});
+    owner=crypto.randomUUID();
+    if(!await store.acquire(owner,now))return {...previous?.payload,pending:!previous?.payload,refreshing:true,fetchError:'PRIVATE_REFRESH_IN_PROGRESS'};
+    const payload=await TIINGO_MARKET.refreshPrivateMarket({token:env.TIINGO_API_TOKEN,expectedSession,now:new Date(now),cache:store.cache,quota:store.quota});
+    if(!await writeRuntimeCache(env,TIINGO_PRIVATE_KEY,payload,370*86400000))throw Error('PRIVATE_CACHE_WRITE_FAILED');
+    return payload;
+  }catch{return {...previous?.payload,pending:false,fetchError:'PRIVATE_REFRESH_FAILED'};}
+  finally{if(store&&owner)try{await store.release(owner);}catch{}}
+}
+async function readUSSectorSnapshot(env={},ctx={},force=false){
+  const fallback=await readYahooSectorSnapshot(env,ctx,force);
+  if(!env.TIINGO_API_TOKEN)return {...fallback,sourceRole:'備援來源',primaryError:'TIINGO_NOT_CONFIGURED'};
+  const saved=await readRuntimeCache(env,TIINGO_PRIVATE_KEY);
+  let payload=saved?.payload;
+  let ready=false;try{const checked=normalizeTiingoSnapshot(payload);ready=TIINGO_MARKET.SYMBOLS.every(id=>checked.status[id].status==='ok');}catch{}
+  const retryAllowed=!payload?.checkedAt||Date.now()-Date.parse(payload.checkedAt)>=3600000;
+  if((force||!ready&&retryAllowed)&&(force||typeof ctx.waitUntil==='function')){
+    if(!tiingoSiteTask)tiingoSiteTask=refreshTiingoSite(env).finally(()=>{tiingoSiteTask=null;});
+    if(force)payload=await tiingoSiteTask;
+    else if(typeof ctx.waitUntil==='function')try{ctx.waitUntil(tiingoSiteTask);}catch{}
+  }
+  let primary;
+  try{primary=normalizeTiingoSnapshot(payload);}catch(error){return {...fallback,sourceRole:'備援來源',primaryError:payload?.fetchError||error.message,tiingoRefreshing:!!tiingoSiteTask};}
+  if(['SPY',...TIINGO_MARKET.SECTORS].some(id=>primary.status[id].status!=='ok'))return {...fallback,sourceRole:'備援來源',primaryError:'TIINGO_PARTIAL_SECTOR_HISTORY',privateBreadthSource:primary};
+  const comparison=compareMarketProviders(primary,fallback);
+  if(comparison.status==='blocked')return {...fallback,sourceRole:'備援來源',primaryError:'TIINGO_CROSS_SOURCE_DIFFERENCE',sourceComparison:comparison};
+  return {...primary,sourceRole:'主要來源',sourceComparison:comparison};
+}
diff --git a/worker/tiingo-store.mjs b/worker/tiingo-store.mjs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..cdc0761
--- /dev/null
+++ b/worker/tiingo-store.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,26 @@
+/** Shared D1 storage; rolling request limits are reserved by ONE atomic SQL UPDATE. */
+export const GUARD_SCHEMA="CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tiingo_private_guard (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK(id=1), requests TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]', symbols TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}', month TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '', lease_owner TEXT, lease_until INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0)";
+export const RESERVE_SQL=`UPDATE tiingo_private_guard SET
+ requests=json_insert(COALESCE((SELECT json_group_array(json(value)) FROM json_each(requests) WHERE json_extract(value,'$.at')>?), '[]'),'$[#]',json(?)),
+ symbols=CASE WHEN month=? THEN json_set(symbols,?,1) ELSE json_object(?,1) END, month=?
+ WHERE id=1 AND (SELECT count(*) FROM json_each(requests) WHERE json_extract(value,'$.at')>?)<50
+ AND (SELECT count(*) FROM json_each(requests) WHERE json_extract(value,'$.at')>?)<1000
+ AND (month<>? OR json_type(symbols,?) IS NOT NULL OR (SELECT count(*) FROM json_each(symbols))<500)
+ RETURNING id`;
+export const LEASE_SQL='UPDATE tiingo_private_guard SET lease_owner=?,lease_until=? WHERE id=1 AND lease_until<=? RETURNING id';
+export async function createPrivateStore({db,read,write}){
+  if(!db?.prepare||!read||!write)throw Error('PRIVATE_STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE');
+  await db.prepare(GUARD_SCHEMA).run();
+  await db.prepare('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO tiingo_private_guard (id) VALUES (1)').run();
+  const change=async(sql,args)=>(await db.prepare(sql).bind(...args).all())?.results?.length>0;
+  return {
+    cache:{get:async key=>(await read(key))?.payload||null,set:async(key,value)=>write(key,value,370*86400000)},
+    quota:{reserve:async(symbol,now)=>{
+      if(!/^[A-Z]{1,8}$/.test(symbol)||!Number.isFinite(now))return false;
+      const month=new Date(now).toISOString().slice(0,7),path=`$.${symbol}`;
+      return change(RESERVE_SQL,[now-86400000,JSON.stringify({at:now,symbol}),month,path,symbol,month,now-3600000,now-86400000,month,path]);
+    }},
+    acquire:(owner,now)=>change(LEASE_SQL,[owner,now+15*60000,now]),
+    release:async owner=>db.prepare('UPDATE tiingo_private_guard SET lease_until=0,lease_owner=NULL WHERE id=1 AND lease_owner=?').bind(owner).run()
+  };
+}

```
